  </div>;
}

function SurveyForm({ assignment, onComplete }: { assignment: SurveyAssignment; onComplete: (time: string) => void }) {
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<SurveyAnswer[]>(() => assignment.definition.items.map(() => null));
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState(''), [uncertain, setUncertain] = useState(false);
  const pending = useRef<{ id: string; answers: string } | null>(null);
  useUnsavedSurvey(answers.some(value => value !== null && value !== ''));
  const min = assignment.definition.minScore ?? 1, max = assignment.definition.maxScore ?? 6;
  const options = Array.from({ length: max - min + 1 }, (_, i) => min + i);
  async function submit() {
    setError('');
    try {
      validateSurveyResponse(answers, assignment.definition);
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(answers);
      if (!pending.current) pending.current = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), answers: serialized };
      if (pending.current.answers !== serialized) throw new Error('正在確認上一筆提交，請重試原作答。');
      setBusy(true);
      const result = await rpc<{ ok: true; submitted_at: string }>('nptc_survey_submit', { body: { assignmentId: assignment.id, version: assignment.scale_version, requestId: pending.current.id, answers } }, AbortSignal.timeout(15000));
      onComplete(result.submitted_at);
    } catch (cause) {
      if (pending.current) setUncertain(true);
      setError((cause as Error).name === 'AbortError' ? '連線逾時，尚無法確認是否已提交。請重試原作答或重新開啟此連結確認。' : (cause as Error).message);
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <form className="survey-form" onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); void submit(); }}>
    <header><h2>{assignment.definition.title}</h2>{assignment.definition.notice && <p className="survey-warning">{assignment.definition.notice}</p>}</header>
    <div className="survey-instructions">{assignment.definition.instructions.map(p => <p key={p}>{p}</p>)}<p>每題選擇一個答案。繳交後保留原始作答，不提供修改。</p></div>
    <fieldset disabled={busy || uncertain} className="survey-items">{assignment.definition.items.map((item, i) => <fieldset className="survey-question" key={i}><legend>第 {i + 1} 題{item.required === false ? '（選填）' : ''}</legend>
      {item.section && <h3 className="survey-domain-heading">{item.section}</h3>}
      {item.text ? <p className="survey-statement-text">{item.text}</p> : <div className="survey-statements"><p><span>左側敘述（{min}）</span>{item.left}</p><p><span>右側敘述（{max}）</span>{item.right}</p></div>}
      {item.type === 'text' ? <textarea aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題文字意見`} rows={4} maxLength={item.maxLength ?? 2000} required={item.required !== false} value={String(answers[i] ?? '')} onChange={event => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? event.target.value : n))}/> : item.type === 'choice' ? <div className="survey-choice-options" role="radiogroup" aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題單選`}>{item.options?.map(value => <label key={value}><input type="radio" name={`survey-q${i + 1}`} value={value} required={item.required !== false} checked={answers[i] === value} onChange={() => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? value : n))}/><span>{value}</span></label>)}</div> : <><div className={`survey-options${assignment.definition.optionLabels ? ' survey-options-labeled' : ''}`} style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${Math.min(options.length, 6)}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }} role="radiogroup" aria-label={`第 ${i + 1} 題，${min} 至 ${max} 單選`}>{options.map(value => <label key={value}><input type="radio" name={`survey-q${i + 1}`} value={value} required={item.required !== false} checked={answers[i] === value} onChange={() => setAnswers(current => current.map((n, j) => i === j ? value : n))} /><span>{value}</span>{assignment.definition.optionLabels?.[value] && <small>{assignment.definition.optionLabels[value]}</small>}</label>)}</div>
      <div className="survey-scale-labels"><span>{assignment.definition.leftLabel ?? (item.left ? '最接近左側' : '')}</span><span>{assignment.definition.rightLabel ?? (item.right ? '最接近右側' : '')}</span></div></>}
    </fieldset>)}</fieldset>
    <div className="survey-submit-bar"><span aria-live="polite">已填 {answers.filter(n => n !== null && n !== '').length} / {answers.length} 題{assignment.definition.items.some(q => q.required === false) && `（${assignment.definition.items.filter(q => q.required === false).length} 題選填）`}</span><Button className="action" disabled={busy} type="submit">{busy ? '繳交中…' : uncertain ? '重試原作答' : '繳交問卷'}</Button></div>
    {uncertain && <p className="survey-muted">為避免重複紀錄，已暫停編輯。重試會使用相同作答與提交編號。</p>}{error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
  </form>;
}
components/survey-analysis.tsx

修改前

import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { formatSurveyTime } from '@/lib/surveys';
import { dimensionLabel, metricRange, metricValue, orderedAssignments, scaleKey, surveyPeople, surveyTrend, type SurveyAnalysisData, type SurveyTrendPoint } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import '@/app/learning-analysis.css';

const scoreText = (value: number | null) => value === null ? '—' : Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
const dateText = (value: string) => value && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-TW', { timeZone: 'Asia/Taipei', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date(value)) : '未設定';

export default function SurveyAnalysis({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<SurveyAnalysisData | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [workshopId, setWorkshopId] = useState(currentWorkshopId ?? 'all'), [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState('');
  const [personId, setPersonId] = useState('all'), [query, setQuery] = useState(''), [metric, setMetric] = useState('total');
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<{ assignmentId: string; studentId: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<SurveyAnalysisData>('nptc_survey_analysis', { requested_workshop: null }, abort.signal)
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [reload]);
  const effectiveWorkshop = data?.workshops.some(w => w.id === workshopId) ? workshopId : 'all';
  const scopeAssignments = useMemo(() => data?.assignments.filter(a => a.status !== 'draft' && (effectiveWorkshop === 'all' || a.workshop_id === effectiveWorkshop)) ?? [], [data, effectiveWorkshop]);
  const scales = [...new Map(scopeAssignments.map(a => [scaleKey(a), { key: scaleKey(a), title: a.definition.title, version: a.scale_version, definition: a.definition }])).values()];
  const scale = scales.find(s => s.key === selectedScale) ?? scales[0];
  const noScores = ['pending', 'unscored'].includes(scale?.definition.scoringStatus ?? '');
  const scoringLabel = scale?.definition.scoringStatus === 'pending' ? '計分規則待確認' : '不計分（原始回覆）';
  const assignments = orderedAssignments(scopeAssignments.filter(a => scale && scaleKey(a) === scale.key));
  const people = data ? surveyPeople(data, assignments) : [];
  const searchedPeople = people.filter(p => p.names.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const person = searchedPeople.find(p => p.identity === personId);
  const displayedPeople = person ? [person] : searchedPeople;
  const metricOptions = noScores ? [{ key: 'unscored', label: scoringLabel }] : [{ key: 'total', label: '總分' }, ...Object.keys(scale?.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(name => ({ key: `dimension:${name}`, label: dimensionLabel(name) }))];
  const activeMetric = metricOptions.some(m => m.key === metric) ? metric : metricOptions[0].key;
  const metricLabel = metricOptions.find(m => m.key === activeMetric)?.label ?? '總分';
  const range = scale ? metricRange(scale.definition, activeMetric) : { min: 0, max: 1 };
  const points = data ? surveyTrend(data, assignments, displayedPeople, activeMetric) : [];
  const identities = new Set(displayedPeople.map(p => p.identity)), assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const responses = data?.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id) && identities.has(r.identity)) ?? [];
  const values = responses.map(r => metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
  const expected = assignments.reduce((sum, a) => sum + displayedPeople.filter(p => p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)).length, 0);
  const detailResponse = detail ? responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === detail.assignmentId && r.student_id === detail.studentId) : undefined;
  const detailAssignment = detailResponse ? assignments.find(a => a.id === detailResponse.assignment_id) : undefined;
  const detailPerson = detailResponse ? displayedPeople.find(p => p.identity === detailResponse.identity) : undefined;
  const resetDetails = () => { setPersonId('all'); setDetail(null); };

  if (!data) return <section className="analysis-state" role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}><BarChart3 size={32}/><h1>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>{error || '正在讀取學員量表紀錄…'}</p>{error && <button className="action" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新載入</button>}</section>;
  return <div className="learning-analysis survey-learning-analysis">
    <header className="analysis-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY ANALYTICS</span><h1><BarChart3 aria-hidden="true"/>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>查詢學員的問卷得分，查看同一份量表的歷次填寫變化。</p></div><button className="action secondary" onClick={() => { setDetail(null); setReload(v => v + 1); }}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true"/>更新量表資料</button></header>
    <section className="analysis-controls" aria-label="量表分析篩選"><div className="survey-analysis-filters">
      <label>OSCE 梯次<select value={effectiveWorkshop} onChange={e => { setWorkshopId(e.target.value); setSelectedScale(''); resetDetails(); }}><option value="all">所有可檢視梯次</option>{data.workshops.map(w => <option value={w.id} key={w.id}>{w.name}{w.archived_at ? '（已封存）' : ''}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>選擇量表<select value={scale?.key ?? ''} disabled={!scales.length} onChange={e => { setSelectedScale(e.target.value); setMetric('total'); resetDetails(); }}>{!scales.length && <option value="">尚無量表發布</option>}{scales.map(s => <option key={s.key} value={s.key}>{s.title} · {s.version}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>查詢學員<input type="search" value={query} placeholder="輸入學員姓名" onChange={e => { setQuery(e.target.value); resetDetails(); }}/></label>
      <label>學員<select value={person?.identity ?? 'all'} onChange={e => { setPersonId(e.target.value); setDetail(null); }}><option value="all">全部符合條件的學員</option>{searchedPeople.map(p => <option key={p.identity} value={p.identity}>{p.names}（{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次）</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>分數項目<select value={activeMetric} disabled={noScores} onChange={e => setMetric(e.target.value)}>{metricOptions.map(m => <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>{m.label}</option>)}</select></label>
    </div><p className="form-help">{data.includesArchived ? '目前可包含已封存梯次。' : '目前僅包含未封存梯次。'}不同量表版本分開呈現；未完成問卷不計為 0 分。</p></section>
    {!assignments.length ? <section className="analysis-panel analysis-empty"><h2>目前尚無可分析的問卷</h2><p>在量表管理庫加入問卷並收回學員作答後，即可查看分數與趨勢。</p></section> : <>
      {scale?.definition.notice && <p className="analysis-note">{scale.definition.notice}</p>}
      <section className="analysis-metrics" aria-label="量表填答摘要"><article><span>學員人數</span><strong>{displayedPeople.length}</strong><small>符合目前篩選條件</small></article><article><span>完成問卷／應填份數</span><strong>{responses.length} / {expected}</strong><small>各次發布分開計算</small></article><article><span>{noScores ? '計分狀態' : `${metricLabel}平均`}</span><strong>{noScores ? '—' : scoreText(values.length ? values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length : null)}</strong><small>{noScores ? scoringLabel : `依 ${values.length} 筆有效分數計算`}</small></article><article><span>同版量表發布次數</span><strong>{assignments.length}</strong><small>{noScores ? '保存原始作答與完成紀錄' : `${metricLabel}量尺範圍 ${range.min}–${range.max} 分`}</small></article></section>
      {noScores ? <section className="analysis-panel" role="status"><h2>{scoringLabel}</h2><p>目前提供完成狀態與逐題原始作答，不產生換算分數、構面分數、總分或分數趨勢。</p></section> : <section className="analysis-panel"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{person ? `${person.names}｜歷次${metricLabel}趨勢` : `各次發布｜${metricLabel}平均趨勢`}</h2><p>依發布時間排列，橫軸使用教師備註；未填備註時以發布次序顯示。</p></div>{person && <button className="analysis-link" onClick={resetDetails}>查看全部學員</button>}</div>
        <SurveyTrendChart points={points} range={range} metricLabel={metricLabel} personal={!!person}/>
        <div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-trend-summary"><thead><tr><th>發布備註</th><th>OSCE 梯次／發布時間</th><th>{person ? metricLabel : `${metricLabel}平均`}</th><th>有效分數筆數</th></tr></thead><tbody>{points.map(p => <tr key={p.id}><th scope="row">{p.label}</th><td>{p.workshop}<small>{formatSurveyTime(p.date)}</small></td><td>{scoreText(p.value)}</td><td>{p.count}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
        <p className="analysis-note">{person ? '折線呈現此學員在各次發布的填答分數；缺少作答的資料點留空。' : '每個資料點是該次發布中符合篩選學員的平均分數，人數可能不同；可選擇單一學員查看個人趨勢。'}分數變化本身不代表教學效果或能力合格判定。</p>
      </section>}
      <section className="analysis-panel" aria-label={noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}><h2>{noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}</h2><p>{noScores ? '點選「查看原答」可閱讀該次問卷的完整原始作答。' : '點選姓名可查看個人趨勢；點選分數可查看該次問卷的逐題原答與換算分數。'}</p><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-student-scores"><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{points.map(p => <th key={p.id}>{p.label}<small>{p.workshop} · {dateText(p.date)}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{displayedPeople.map(p => <tr key={p.identity}><th scope="row"><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail(null); }}>{p.names}</button><small>{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次紀錄</small></th>{assignments.map(a => {
        if (!p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)) return <td key={a.id}><span className="survey-muted">不適用</span></td>;
        const r = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.identity === p.identity), value = r ? metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric) : null;
        return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><button className="survey-score-button" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail({ assignmentId: a.id, studentId: r.student_id }); }}>{noScores ? '查看原答' : value === null ? '已完成' : `${scoreText(value)} 分`}</button><small>填寫：{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">尚未填寫</span>}</td>;
      })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!displayedPeople.length && <p className="analysis-empty">目前沒有符合條件的學員。</p>}</section>
      {detailResponse && detailAssignment && <section className="analysis-panel survey-answer-detail" aria-label="逐題量表作答"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{detailPerson?.names}｜逐題作答</h2><p>{detailAssignment.definition.title} · {detailAssignment.note || '未填備註'} · {formatSurveyTime(detailResponse.submitted_at)}</p></div><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => setDetail(null)}>關閉明細</button></div><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>題號</th><th>題目內容</th><th>原始作答</th><th>換算得分</th></tr></thead><tbody>{detailAssignment.definition.items.map((q, i) => <tr key={i}><th scope="row">{i + 1}</th><td className="survey-answer-statement">{q.text || <><p>左：{q.left}</p><p>右：{q.right}</p></>}</td><td>{detailResponse.answers[i] ?? '—'}</td><td>{noScores ? scoringLabel : detailResponse.scores.itemScores[i] ?? '—'}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><p className="analysis-note">{noScores ? scoringLabel : `總分：${scoreText(detailResponse.scores.total)}`}。資料以該次問卷保存的題目與計分版本呈現。</p></section>}
    </>}
  </div>;
}

function SurveyTrendChart({ points, range, metricLabel, personal }: { points: SurveyTrendPoint[]; range: { min: number; max: number }; metricLabel: string; personal: boolean }) {
  const id = useId();
  const filled = points.filter(p => p.value !== null);
  if (!filled.length) return <p className="analysis-empty" role="status">目前沒有可繪製的{metricLabel}紀錄，尚未填答不會畫成 0 分。</p>;
  const width = 840, height = 316, left = 64, right = 30, top = 32, bottom = 72;
  const low = Math.min(range.min, ...filled.map(p => p.value!)), high = Math.max(range.max, ...filled.map(p => p.value!));
  const span = high - low || 1;
  const x = (i: number) => left + (points.length === 1 ? (width - left - right) / 2 : i * (width - left - right) / (points.length - 1));
  const y = (value: number) => top + (high - value) * (height - top - bottom) / span;
  const segments: { x: number; y: number }[][] = [];
  let segment: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
  points.forEach((p, i) => { if (p.value === null) { if (segment.length) segments.push(segment); segment = []; } else segment.push({ x: x(i), y: y(p.value) }); });
  if (segment.length) segments.push(segment);
  const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(points.length / 8));
  return <figure className="survey-trend-figure"><div className="survey-trend-chart-scroll"><svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-labelledby={`${id}-title ${id}-desc`}><title id={`${id}-title`}>{personal ? '學員' : '各次發布平均'}{metricLabel}趨勢</title><desc id={`${id}-desc`}>{filled.length} 個有效資料點。完整數值列於下方表格，缺漏紀錄不連線。</desc>
    {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(i => { const value = low + span * i / 4; return <g key={i}><line x1={left} x2={width - right} y1={y(value)} y2={y(value)} stroke="#dce7dc" strokeDasharray={i === 0 ? undefined : '4 5'}/><text x={left - 13} y={y(value) + 4} textAnchor="end" fill="#5c7368" fontSize={12}>{scoreText(value)}</text></g>; })}
    <text x={left} y={16} fill="#365d4c" fontSize={12}>{metricLabel}（分）</text>
    {segments.filter(s => s.length > 1).map((s, i) => <polyline key={i} fill="none" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3} strokeLinejoin="round" points={s.map(p => `${p.x},${p.y}`).join(' ')}/>)}
    {points.map((p, i) => <g key={p.id}>{p.value !== null && <g className="survey-trend-point" tabIndex={0} role="img" aria-label={`${p.label}，${metricLabel} ${scoreText(p.value)}，${p.count} 筆`}><title>{p.label}｜{p.workshop}｜{scoreText(p.value)} 分｜n={p.count}</title><circle cx={x(i)} cy={y(p.value)} r={6} fill="#fff" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3}/>{points.length <= 14 && <text x={x(i)} y={y(p.value) - 13} textAnchor="middle" fill="#164a38" fontSize={12} fontWeight={600}>{scoreText(p.value)}</text>}</g>}{(i % step === 0 || i === points.length - 1) && <><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 24} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#345a49" fontSize={11}>{p.label.length > 10 ? `${p.label.slice(0, 9)}…` : p.label}</text><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 42} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#728478" fontSize={11}>{dateText(p.date)}</text></>}</g>)}
  </svg></div><figcaption>{filled.length === 1 ? '目前僅有一筆有效紀錄，暫以單一資料點呈現。' : '依同份同版量表的發布順序呈現。'}圖表下方可查看各次分數與人數。</figcaption></figure>;
}

修改後

import { useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { BarChart3, RefreshCw } from 'lucide-react';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { formatSurveyTime } from '@/lib/surveys';
import { dimensionLabel, metricRange, metricValue, orderedAssignments, scaleKey, surveyPeople, surveyTrend, type SurveyAnalysisData, type SurveyAnalysisResponse, type SurveyTrendPoint } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import { requireSurveySchema } from '@/lib/survey-schema';
import '@/app/learning-analysis.css';

const scoreText = (value: number | null) => value === null ? '—' : Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(1);
const dateText = (value: string) => value && Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)) ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat('zh-TW', { timeZone: 'Asia/Taipei', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).format(new Date(value)) : '未設定';

export default function SurveyAnalysis({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<SurveyAnalysisData | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [workshopId, setWorkshopId] = useState(currentWorkshopId ?? ''), [selectedScale, setSelectedScale] = useState('');
  const [workshopsReady, setWorkshopsReady] = useState(false);
  const [personId, setPersonId] = useState('all'), [query, setQuery] = useState(''), [metric, setMetric] = useState('total');
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<{ assignmentId: string; studentId: string } | null>(null);
  const [answerDetail, setAnswerDetail] = useState<SurveyAnalysisResponse | null>(null), [detailError, setDetailError] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setWorkshopsReady(false); setData(null); setError('');
    requireSurveySchema(abort.signal).then(() => rpc<Pick<SurveyAnalysisData, 'workshops'>>('nptc_survey_analysis_workshops', {}, abort.signal))
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) { setWorkshopId(current => current === 'all' || result.workshops.some(w => w.id === current) ? current : result.workshops[0]?.id ?? 'all'); setWorkshopsReady(true); } })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [reload]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!workshopsReady) return;
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<SurveyAnalysisData>('nptc_survey_analysis_summary', { requested_workshop: workshopId === 'all' ? null : workshopId }, abort.signal)
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [reload, workshopId, workshopsReady]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setAnswerDetail(null); setDetailError('');
    if (!detail) return;
    const abort = new AbortController();
    rpc<SurveyAnalysisResponse>('nptc_survey_response_detail', { requested_assignment: detail.assignmentId, requested_student: detail.studentId }, abort.signal)
      .then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setAnswerDetail(result); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setDetailError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [detail]);
  const effectiveWorkshop = data?.workshops.some(w => w.id === workshopId) ? workshopId : 'all';
  const scopeAssignments = useMemo(() => data?.assignments.filter(a => a.status !== 'draft' && (effectiveWorkshop === 'all' || a.workshop_id === effectiveWorkshop)) ?? [], [data, effectiveWorkshop]);
  const scales = [...new Map(scopeAssignments.map(a => [scaleKey(a), { key: scaleKey(a), title: a.definition.title, version: a.scale_version, definition: a.definition }])).values()];
  const scale = scales.find(s => s.key === selectedScale) ?? scales[0];
  const noScores = ['pending', 'unscored'].includes(scale?.definition.scoringStatus ?? '');
  const scoringLabel = scale?.definition.scoringStatus === 'pending' ? '計分規則待確認' : '不計分（原始回覆）';
  const assignments = orderedAssignments(scopeAssignments.filter(a => scale && scaleKey(a) === scale.key));
  const people = data ? surveyPeople(data, assignments) : [];
  const searchedPeople = people.filter(p => p.names.toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()));
  const person = searchedPeople.find(p => p.identity === personId);
  const displayedPeople = person ? [person] : searchedPeople;
  const metricOptions = noScores ? [{ key: 'unscored', label: scoringLabel }] : [{ key: 'total', label: '總分' }, ...Object.keys(scale?.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(name => ({ key: `dimension:${name}`, label: dimensionLabel(name) }))];
  const activeMetric = metricOptions.some(m => m.key === metric) ? metric : metricOptions[0].key;
  const metricLabel = metricOptions.find(m => m.key === activeMetric)?.label ?? '總分';
  const range = scale ? metricRange(scale.definition, activeMetric) : { min: 0, max: 1 };
  const points = data ? surveyTrend(data, assignments, displayedPeople, activeMetric) : [];
  const identities = new Set(displayedPeople.map(p => p.identity)), assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const responses = data?.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id) && identities.has(r.identity)) ?? [];
  const values = responses.map(r => metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
  const expected = assignments.reduce((sum, a) => sum + displayedPeople.filter(p => p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)).length, 0);
  const detailResponse = detail && answerDetail?.assignment_id === detail.assignmentId && answerDetail.student_id === detail.studentId && responses.some(r => r.assignment_id === detail.assignmentId && r.student_id === detail.studentId) ? answerDetail : undefined;
  const detailAssignment = detailResponse ? assignments.find(a => a.id === detailResponse.assignment_id) : undefined;
  const detailPerson = detailResponse ? displayedPeople.find(p => p.identity === detailResponse.identity) : undefined;
  const resetDetails = () => { setPersonId('all'); setDetail(null); };

  if (!data) return <section className="analysis-state" role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}><BarChart3 size={32}/><h1>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>{error || '正在讀取學員量表紀錄…'}</p>{error && <button className="action" onClick={() => setReload(v => v + 1)}>重新載入</button>}</section>;
  return <div className="learning-analysis survey-learning-analysis">
    <header className="analysis-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY ANALYTICS</span><h1><BarChart3 aria-hidden="true"/>量表分數與趨勢</h1><p>查詢學員的問卷得分，查看同一份量表的歷次填寫變化。</p></div><button className="action secondary" onClick={() => { setDetail(null); setReload(v => v + 1); }}><RefreshCw size={16} aria-hidden="true"/>更新量表資料</button></header>
    <section className="analysis-controls" aria-label="量表分析篩選"><div className="survey-analysis-filters">
      <label>OSCE 梯次<select value={effectiveWorkshop} onChange={e => { setWorkshopId(e.target.value); setSelectedScale(''); resetDetails(); }}><option value="all">所有可檢視梯次</option>{data.workshops.map(w => <option value={w.id} key={w.id}>{w.name}{w.archived_at ? '（已封存）' : ''}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>選擇量表<select value={scale?.key ?? ''} disabled={!scales.length} onChange={e => { setSelectedScale(e.target.value); setMetric('total'); resetDetails(); }}>{!scales.length && <option value="">尚無量表發布</option>}{scales.map(s => <option key={s.key} value={s.key}>{s.title} · {s.version}</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>查詢學員<input type="search" value={query} placeholder="輸入學員姓名" onChange={e => { setQuery(e.target.value); resetDetails(); }}/></label>
      <label>學員<select value={person?.identity ?? 'all'} onChange={e => { setPersonId(e.target.value); setDetail(null); }}><option value="all">全部符合條件的學員</option>{searchedPeople.map(p => <option key={p.identity} value={p.identity}>{p.names}（{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次）</option>)}</select></label>
      <label>分數項目<select value={activeMetric} disabled={noScores} onChange={e => setMetric(e.target.value)}>{metricOptions.map(m => <option key={m.key} value={m.key}>{m.label}</option>)}</select></label>
    </div><p className="form-help">{data.includesArchived ? '目前可包含已封存梯次。' : '目前僅包含未封存梯次。'}不同量表版本分開呈現；未完成問卷不計為 0 分。</p></section>
    {!assignments.length ? <section className="analysis-panel analysis-empty"><h2>目前尚無可分析的問卷</h2><p>在量表管理庫加入問卷並收回學員作答後，即可查看分數與趨勢。</p></section> : <>
      {scale?.definition.notice && <p className="analysis-note">{scale.definition.notice}</p>}
      <section className="analysis-metrics" aria-label="量表填答摘要"><article><span>學員人數</span><strong>{displayedPeople.length}</strong><small>符合目前篩選條件</small></article><article><span>完成問卷／應填份數</span><strong>{responses.length} / {expected}</strong><small>各次發布分開計算</small></article><article><span>{noScores ? '計分狀態' : `${metricLabel}平均`}</span><strong>{noScores ? '—' : scoreText(values.length ? values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / values.length : null)}</strong><small>{noScores ? scoringLabel : `依 ${values.length} 筆有效分數計算`}</small></article><article><span>同版量表發布次數</span><strong>{assignments.length}</strong><small>{noScores ? '保存原始作答與完成紀錄' : `${metricLabel}量尺範圍 ${range.min}–${range.max} 分`}</small></article></section>
      {noScores ? <section className="analysis-panel" role="status"><h2>{scoringLabel}</h2><p>目前提供完成狀態與逐題原始作答，不產生換算分數、構面分數、總分或分數趨勢。</p></section> : <section className="analysis-panel"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{person ? `${person.names}｜歷次${metricLabel}趨勢` : `各次發布｜${metricLabel}平均趨勢`}</h2><p>依發布時間排列，橫軸使用教師備註；未填備註時以發布次序顯示。</p></div>{person && <button className="analysis-link" onClick={resetDetails}>查看全部學員</button>}</div>
        <SurveyTrendChart points={points} range={range} metricLabel={metricLabel} personal={!!person}/>
        <div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-trend-summary"><thead><tr><th>發布備註</th><th>OSCE 梯次／發布時間</th><th>{person ? metricLabel : `${metricLabel}平均`}</th><th>有效分數筆數</th></tr></thead><tbody>{points.map(p => <tr key={p.id}><th scope="row">{p.label}</th><td>{p.workshop}<small>{formatSurveyTime(p.date)}</small></td><td>{scoreText(p.value)}</td><td>{p.count}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
        <p className="analysis-note">{person ? '折線呈現此學員在各次發布的填答分數；缺少作答的資料點留空。' : '每個資料點是該次發布中符合篩選學員的平均分數，人數可能不同；可選擇單一學員查看個人趨勢。'}分數變化本身不代表教學效果或能力合格判定。</p>
      </section>}
      <section className="analysis-panel" aria-label={noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}><h2>{noScores ? '學員原始作答' : '學員量表分數'}</h2><p>{noScores ? '點選「查看原答」可閱讀該次問卷的完整原始作答。' : '點選姓名可查看個人趨勢；點選分數可查看該次問卷的逐題原答與換算分數。'}</p><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table className="survey-student-scores"><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{points.map(p => <th key={p.id}>{p.label}<small>{p.workshop} · {dateText(p.date)}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{displayedPeople.map(p => <tr key={p.identity}><th scope="row"><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail(null); }}>{p.names}</button><small>{p.workshopIds.length} 個梯次紀錄</small></th>{assignments.map(a => {
        if (!p.workshopIds.includes(a.workshop_id)) return <td key={a.id}><span className="survey-muted">不適用</span></td>;
        const r = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.identity === p.identity), value = r ? metricValue(r.scores, activeMetric) : null;
        return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><button className="survey-score-button" onClick={() => { setPersonId(p.identity); setDetail({ assignmentId: a.id, studentId: r.student_id }); }}>{noScores ? '查看原答' : value === null ? '已完成' : `${scoreText(value)} 分`}</button><small>填寫：{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">尚未填寫</span>}</td>;
      })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!displayedPeople.length && <p className="analysis-empty">目前沒有符合條件的學員。</p>}</section>
      {detail && !detailResponse && <p role={detailError ? 'alert' : 'status'}>{detailError || '正在讀取此筆原始作答…'}</p>}
      {detailResponse && detailAssignment && <section className="analysis-panel survey-answer-detail" aria-label="逐題量表作答"><div className="analysis-panel-heading"><div><h2>{detailPerson?.names}｜逐題作答</h2><p>{detailAssignment.definition.title} · {detailAssignment.note || '未填備註'} · {formatSurveyTime(detailResponse.submitted_at)}</p></div><button className="analysis-link" onClick={() => setDetail(null)}>關閉明細</button></div><div className="analysis-table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>題號</th><th>題目內容</th><th>原始作答</th><th>換算得分</th></tr></thead><tbody>{detailAssignment.definition.items.map((q, i) => <tr key={i}><th scope="row">{i + 1}</th><td className="survey-answer-statement">{q.text || <><p>左：{q.left}</p><p>右：{q.right}</p></>}</td><td>{detailResponse.answers?.[i] ?? '—'}</td><td>{noScores ? scoringLabel : detailResponse.scores.itemScores[i] ?? '—'}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div><p className="analysis-note">{noScores ? scoringLabel : `總分：${scoreText(detailResponse.scores.total)}`}。資料以該次問卷保存的題目與計分版本呈現。</p></section>}
    </>}
  </div>;
}

function SurveyTrendChart({ points, range, metricLabel, personal }: { points: SurveyTrendPoint[]; range: { min: number; max: number }; metricLabel: string; personal: boolean }) {
  const id = useId();
  const filled = points.filter(p => p.value !== null);
  if (!filled.length) return <p className="analysis-empty" role="status">目前沒有可繪製的{metricLabel}紀錄，尚未填答不會畫成 0 分。</p>;
  const width = 840, height = 316, left = 64, right = 30, top = 32, bottom = 72;
  const low = Math.min(range.min, ...filled.map(p => p.value!)), high = Math.max(range.max, ...filled.map(p => p.value!));
  const span = high - low || 1;
  const x = (i: number) => left + (points.length === 1 ? (width - left - right) / 2 : i * (width - left - right) / (points.length - 1));
  const y = (value: number) => top + (high - value) * (height - top - bottom) / span;
  const segments: { x: number; y: number }[][] = [];
  let segment: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
  points.forEach((p, i) => { if (p.value === null) { if (segment.length) segments.push(segment); segment = []; } else segment.push({ x: x(i), y: y(p.value) }); });
  if (segment.length) segments.push(segment);
  const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(points.length / 8));
  return <figure className="survey-trend-figure"><div className="survey-trend-chart-scroll"><svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-labelledby={`${id}-title ${id}-desc`}><title id={`${id}-title`}>{personal ? '學員' : '各次發布平均'}{metricLabel}趨勢</title><desc id={`${id}-desc`}>{filled.length} 個有效資料點。完整數值列於下方表格，缺漏紀錄不連線。</desc>
    {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map(i => { const value = low + span * i / 4; return <g key={i}><line x1={left} x2={width - right} y1={y(value)} y2={y(value)} stroke="#dce7dc" strokeDasharray={i === 0 ? undefined : '4 5'}/><text x={left - 13} y={y(value) + 4} textAnchor="end" fill="#5c7368" fontSize={12}>{scoreText(value)}</text></g>; })}
    <text x={left} y={16} fill="#365d4c" fontSize={12}>{metricLabel}（分）</text>
    {segments.filter(s => s.length > 1).map((s, i) => <polyline key={i} fill="none" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3} strokeLinejoin="round" points={s.map(p => `${p.x},${p.y}`).join(' ')}/>)}
    {points.map((p, i) => <g key={p.id}>{p.value !== null && <g className="survey-trend-point" tabIndex={0} role="img" aria-label={`${p.label}，${metricLabel} ${scoreText(p.value)}，${p.count} 筆`}><title>{p.label}｜{p.workshop}｜{scoreText(p.value)} 分｜n={p.count}</title><circle cx={x(i)} cy={y(p.value)} r={6} fill="#fff" stroke="#2c7962" strokeWidth={3}/>{points.length <= 14 && <text x={x(i)} y={y(p.value) - 13} textAnchor="middle" fill="#164a38" fontSize={12} fontWeight={600}>{scoreText(p.value)}</text>}</g>}{(i % step === 0 || i === points.length - 1) && <><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 24} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#345a49" fontSize={11}>{p.label.length > 10 ? `${p.label.slice(0, 9)}…` : p.label}</text><text x={x(i)} y={height - bottom + 42} textAnchor={points.length === 1 ? 'middle' : i === 0 ? 'start' : i === points.length - 1 ? 'end' : 'middle'} fill="#728478" fontSize={11}>{dateText(p.date)}</text></>}</g>)}
  </svg></div><figcaption>{filled.length === 1 ? '目前僅有一筆有效紀錄，暫以單一資料點呈現。' : '依同份同版量表的發布順序呈現。'}圖表下方可查看各次分數與人數。</figcaption></figure>;
}
components/teacher-surveys.tsx

修改前

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from '@/components/ui/native-select';
import { SurveyAssignDialog } from '@/components/survey-assign-dialog';
import { DeleteSurveyDialog } from '@/components/delete-survey-dialog';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { appUrl } from '@/lib/routes';
import { shortSurveyPath } from '@/lib/survey-short-link';
import { downloadSurveyQr } from '@/lib/survey-qr';
import { dimensionLabel } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import { downloadSurveyCsv, formatSurveyTime, surveyResponsesCsv, taipeiInput, taipeiToUtc, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveyDefinition, type SurveyExport, type SurveyInstrument, type SurveyScores, type SurveyStatus } from '@/lib/surveys';
import '@/app/surveys.css';

type Workshop = { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null };
type Data = { assignments: SurveyAssignment[]; students: { id: string; name: string }[]; responses: { assignment_id: string; student_id: string; submitted_at: string; scores: SurveyScores }[] };
const answerUrl = (id: string) => appUrl(shortSurveyPath(id));
const itemScoringLabel = (definition: SurveyDefinition, index: number) => definition.scoringStatus === 'unscored' ? '原始回覆，不計分' : definition.scoringStatus === 'pending' ? `${definition.negativeItems?.includes(index + 1) ? '負向敘述；' : ''}計分規則待確認` : definition.reverseItems?.includes(index + 1) ? '反向計分' : '正向計分';

export default function TeacherSurveys({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [workshops, setWorkshops] = useState<Workshop[]>([]), [selected, setSelected] = useState('');
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<SurveyInstrument[]>([]), [libraryLoading, setLibraryLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState(''), [message, setMessage] = useState(''), [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);
  const [view, setView] = useState<'assignments' | 'library'>('library'), [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [expandedWorkshop, setExpandedWorkshop] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [assigningWorkshop, setAssigningWorkshop] = useState<Workshop | null>(null);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState<{ instrument: SurveyInstrument; requestId: string } | null>(null);
  const workshop = workshops.find(w => w.id === selected);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController();
    rpc<{ workshops: Workshop[] }>('nptc_analysis_data', {}, abort.signal).then(result => {
      if (abort.signal.aborted) return;
      setWorkshops(result.workshops);
      setSelected(current => result.workshops.some(w => w.id === current) ? current : result.workshops.some(w => w.id === currentWorkshopId) ? currentWorkshopId! : result.workshops.find(w => !w.archived_at)?.id ?? '');
    }).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [currentWorkshopId, revision]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setLibraryLoading(true);
    rpc<{ instruments: SurveyInstrument[] }>('nptc_survey_library', {}, abort.signal).then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setLibrary(result.instruments); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setLibraryLoading(false); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [revision]);
  async function write(name: string, body: Record<string, unknown>, notice = '設定已儲存。') {
    setBusy(true); setError(''); setMessage('');
    try { await rpc(name, { body }); setMessage(notice); setRevision(v => v + 1); return true; }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); return false; }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  async function share(id: string, native = false) {
    setError('');
    try {
      if (native && navigator.share) await navigator.share({ title: '學員問卷填寫', url: answerUrl(id) });
      else { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(answerUrl(id)); setMessage('問卷連結已複製，可貼到課程群組或訊息中傳送。學員登入後即可填寫這份問卷。'); }
    } catch (cause) { if ((cause as Error).name !== 'AbortError') setError('連結未複製；可選取問卷卡片中的網址手動複製。'); }
  }
  async function exportData(workshopId: string, id?: string) {
    setBusy(true); setError('');
    try {
      const result = await rpc<SurveyExport>('nptc_survey_export', { requested_workshop: workshopId });
      if (id) result.responses = result.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === id);
      downloadSurveyCsv(surveyResponsesCsv(result), `NPTC_量表作答_${id ?? workshopId}.csv`);
      setMessage('研究資料已匯出，包含問卷編號、教師備註、原答與分數；不含姓名、Email 或手機。');
    } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <div className="survey-admin">
    <header className="teacher-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY MANAGEMENT</span><h1>量表管理庫</h1><p>在量表庫查看內容，於發布管理依 OSCE 梯次管理問卷。</p></div></header>
    {error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setError(''); setRevision(v => v + 1); }}>重新讀取</Button></p>}
    {message && <p className="notice success" role="status">{message}</p>}
    <nav className="survey-view-tabs" aria-label="量表管理庫分頁"><button disabled={busy} aria-current={view === 'library' ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setView('library')}>量表庫</button><button disabled={busy} aria-current={view === 'assignments' ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setView('assignments')}>發布管理</button></nav>
    {view === 'library' && <section aria-label="量表庫">
      <div className="survey-library-heading"><div><h2>量表庫</h2><p className="survey-muted">展開查看完整題目，選擇量表後再指定加入的 OSCE 梯次。</p></div><label>搜尋量表<input type="search" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="輸入量表名稱" /></label></div>
      {libraryLoading && <p role="status">正在載入量表庫…</p>}
      {!libraryLoading && library.length === 0 && <p className="empty-panel">量表庫目前尚無可使用的量表。</p>}
      <div className="survey-library-list">{library.filter(i => `${i.definition.title} ${i.version}`.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())).map(instrument => <article className="survey-card" key={`${instrument.code}-${instrument.version}`}>
        <div className="survey-library-title"><div><h3>{instrument.definition.title}</h3><p className="survey-muted">{instrument.definition.items.length} 題 · {instrument.definition.items.some(q => q.type === 'text' || q.type === 'choice') ? '單選與文字回覆' : `${instrument.definition.minScore ?? 1}–${instrument.definition.maxScore ?? 6} 分`} · {instrument.version}</p></div><Button className="action" disabled={busy || !workshops.some(w => !w.archived_at)} onClick={() => { if (workshop?.archived_at) setSelected(workshops.find(w => !w.archived_at)?.id ?? ''); setAdding({ instrument, requestId: crypto.randomUUID() }); }}>加入梯次</Button></div>
        {instrument.definition.notice && <p className="survey-warning">{instrument.definition.notice}</p>}
        <details className="survey-library-content"><summary>展開量表內容</summary>{instrument.definition.instructions.map(p => <p key={p}>{p}</p>)}{!!Object.keys(instrument.definition.dimensions ?? {}).length && <p className="survey-muted">構面：{Object.entries(instrument.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(([name, items]) => `${dimensionLabel(name)}（${items.length} 題）`).join('、')}</p>}<ol>{instrument.definition.items.map((q, i) => <li key={i}>{q.section && <h4>{q.section}</h4>}{q.text ? <p>{q.text}</p> : <div className="survey-statements"><p><span>左側敘述</span>{q.left}</p><p><span>右側敘述</span>{q.right}</p></div>}{q.options && <p className="survey-muted">選項：{q.options.join('／')}</p>}{q.type === 'text' && <p className="survey-muted">文字意見，{q.required === false ? '選填' : '必填'}</p>}<small className="survey-muted">{itemScoringLabel(instrument.definition,i)}</small></li>)}</ol></details>
        {adding?.instrument === instrument && <form className="survey-add-form" onSubmit={async event => {
          event.preventDefault(); const fields = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
          if (!selected || workshop?.archived_at) { setError('請選擇可發布的 OSCE 梯次。'); return; }
          if (await write('nptc_survey_assign', { workshopId: selected, scaleCode: instrument.code, scaleVersion: instrument.version, requestId: adding.requestId, note: String(fields.get('note') ?? ''), status: fields.get('publish') ? 'open' : 'draft' }, '量表已加入 OSCE 梯次，可在發布管理中複製填答連結。')) { setAdding(null); setExpandedWorkshop(selected); setView('assignments'); }
        }}><label>加入 OSCE 梯次<NativeSelect required aria-label="加入量表的 OSCE 梯次" value={workshop?.archived_at ? '' : selected} disabled={busy} onChange={e => setSelected(e.target.value)}><NativeSelectOption value="">請選擇梯次</NativeSelectOption>{workshops.filter(w => !w.archived_at).map(w => <NativeSelectOption key={w.id} value={w.id}>{w.name}</NativeSelectOption>)}</NativeSelect></label><label>備註（僅老師可見）<textarea name="note" maxLength={2000} rows={2} placeholder="自行註記用途、日期或施測安排" /></label><label className="survey-check-label"><input name="publish" type="checkbox" defaultChecked />加入後立即開放填答</label><div className="survey-export-actions"><Button className="action" type="submit" disabled={busy}>確認加入量表</Button><Button className="action secondary" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setAdding(null)}>取消</Button></div></form>}
      </article>)}</div>
    </section>}
    {view === 'assignments' && <section aria-label="發布管理"><div className="survey-library-heading"><div><h2>發布管理</h2><p className="survey-muted">點選 OSCE 梯次，展開該梯次的問卷、填答連結及完成紀錄。</p></div><Button className="action secondary small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setRevision(v => v + 1)}>更新發布紀錄</Button></div>
      {!workshops.length && <p className="empty-panel">目前沒有 OSCE 梯次，請先建立梯次與學員名冊。</p>}
      <div className="survey-workshop-list">{workshops.map(w => <article className={`survey-workshop-group${expandedWorkshop === w.id ? ' expanded' : ''}`} key={w.id}>
        <button className="survey-workshop-toggle" type="button" disabled={busy} aria-expanded={expandedWorkshop === w.id} aria-controls={`survey-publishing-${w.id}`} onClick={() => { setExpandedWorkshop(current => current === w.id ? null : w.id); setSelected(w.id); setAdding(null); }}><span className="survey-workshop-symbol" aria-hidden="true">▤</span><span className="survey-workshop-copy"><strong>{w.name}</strong><small>{w.archived_at ? '已封存，可查看問卷與回收紀錄' : '查看此梯次的問卷、填答連結與完成紀錄'}</small></span><span className="survey-workshop-open-label">{expandedWorkshop === w.id ? '收合內容' : '查看發布內容'}<span className="survey-workshop-arrow" aria-hidden="true">{expandedWorkshop === w.id ? '⌃' : '⌄'}</span></span></button>
        {expandedWorkshop === w.id && <div id={`survey-publishing-${w.id}`} className="survey-workshop-content"><WorkshopPublishing workshop={w} revision={revision} busy={busy} onAdd={() => { setError(''); setAssigningWorkshop(w); }} onWrite={write} onShare={share} onExport={id => exportData(w.id, id)} onDeleted={() => { setMessage('本次問卷發布已刪除。'); setRevision(v => v + 1); }} /></div>}
      </article>)}</div>
    </section>}
    {assigningWorkshop && <SurveyAssignDialog workshop={assigningWorkshop} instruments={library} busy={busy} error={error} onClose={() => setAssigningWorkshop(null)} onSave={body => write('nptc_survey_assign', body, '量表已加入本梯次，發布內容已更新。')}/>}
  </div>;
}

function WorkshopPublishing({ workshop, revision, busy, onAdd, onWrite, onShare, onExport, onDeleted }: { workshop: Workshop; revision: number; busy: boolean; onAdd: () => void; onWrite: (name: string, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<boolean>; onShare: (id: string, native?: boolean) => void; onExport: (id?: string) => void; onDeleted: () => void }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Data | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<Data>('nptc_survey_teacher_data', { requested_workshop: workshop.id }, abort.signal).then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); }).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [workshop.id, revision, retry]);
  return <>
    {workshop.archived_at ? <p className="notice">此梯次已封存，僅供檢視及研究匯出。</p> : <Button className="action" disabled={busy} onClick={onAdd}>＋ 加入量表</Button>}
    {error ? <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setRetry(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p> : !data ? <p role="status">正在讀取本梯次發布內容…</p> : !data.assignments.length ? <p className="survey-empty survey-muted">本梯次尚未加入量表，請點選「加入量表」。</p> : <>
      <div className="survey-cards">{data.assignments.map(a => <AssignmentSettings key={`${a.id}-${a.revision}`} assignment={a} count={data.students.length} disabled={busy || !!workshop.archived_at} onSave={body => onWrite('nptc_survey_update', body)} onCopy={() => onShare(a.id)} onShare={() => onShare(a.id, true)} onExport={() => onExport(a.id)} onDeleted={onDeleted} />)}</div>
      <section className="data-panel survey-results"><div className="panel-heading"><div><h2>問卷完成情形</h2><p className="form-help">學員端只顯示填寫狀態；老師可至學習分析儀表板查詢分數與歷次趨勢。</p></div></div><div className="survey-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{data.assignments.map(a => <th key={a.id}>{a.definition.title}<small className="survey-muted">{a.note || '未填備註'}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{data.students.map(s => <tr key={s.id}><td>{s.name}</td>{data.assignments.map(a => { const r = data.responses.find(r => r.student_id === s.id && r.assignment_id === a.id); return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><strong>已完成</strong><small className="survey-muted">{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">未完成</span>}</td>; })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!data.students.length && <p className="survey-muted">本梯次尚無學員，請先建立名冊。</p>}<div className="survey-export-actions"><Button className="action" disabled={busy || !data.responses.length} onClick={() => onExport()}>匯出本梯次全部問卷作答</Button></div></section>
    </>}
  </>;
}

function AssignmentSettings({ assignment: a, count, disabled, onSave, onCopy, onShare, onExport, onDeleted }: { assignment: SurveyAssignment; count: number; disabled: boolean; onSave: (body: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<boolean>; onCopy: () => void; onShare: () => void; onExport: () => void; onDeleted: () => void }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<SurveyStatus>(a.status === 'draft' ? 'open' : a.status), [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [qrBusy, setQrBusy] = useState(false);
  async function downloadQr() {
    setQrBusy(true); setError('');
    try { await downloadSurveyQr(answerUrl(a.id), a.scale_code, a.id); }
    catch { setError('QR code 下載失敗，請再試一次。'); }
    finally { setQrBusy(false); }
  }
  const linkRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  return <article className="survey-card"><div className="survey-card-heading"><span className="survey-badge">{a.status === 'draft' ? '草稿' : a.status === 'open' ? '已發布' : '已關閉'}</span><span className="survey-muted">已完成 {a.submitted_count ?? 0} / {count} 人</span></div><h2>{a.definition.title}</h2>
    <div className="survey-link-box"><label>學員填答短網址<input ref={linkRef} readOnly value={answerUrl(a.id)} onFocus={e => e.currentTarget.select()} /></label><div className="survey-export-actions"><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" onClick={onCopy}>複製短網址</Button><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" onClick={onShare}>分享連結</Button><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" disabled={qrBusy} onClick={downloadQr}>{qrBusy ? '正在產生 QR code…' : '下載 QR code'}</Button><a className="text-link" href={answerUrl(a.id)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">開啟問卷 ↗</a></div></div>
    <form onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); setError(''); const fields = new FormData(event.currentTarget); try { const opensAt = taipeiToUtc(String(fields.get('opensAt') ?? '')), closesAt = taipeiToUtc(String(fields.get('closesAt') ?? '')); if (opensAt && closesAt && opensAt >= closesAt) throw new Error('截止時間須晚於開放時間。'); void onSave({ id: a.id, revision: a.revision, status, note: String(fields.get('note') ?? ''), opensAt, closesAt }); } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); } }}>
      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="survey-settings"><label>備註（僅老師可見）<textarea name="note" maxLength={2000} rows={2} defaultValue={a.note} /></label><label>發布狀態<NativeSelect value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value as SurveyStatus)}><NativeSelectOption value="open">開放填答</NativeSelectOption><NativeSelectOption value="closed">關閉填答</NativeSelectOption></NativeSelect></label><label>開放時間（臺灣時間，可留空）<input type="datetime-local" name="opensAt" defaultValue={taipeiInput(a.opens_at)} /></label><label>截止時間（臺灣時間，可留空）<input type="datetime-local" name="closesAt" defaultValue={taipeiInput(a.closes_at)} /></label><Button className="action" type="submit">{a.status === 'draft' ? '儲存並發布' : '儲存設定'}</Button></fieldset>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="notice error">{error}</p>}
    </form><div className="survey-assignment-footer"><Button className="action secondary small" disabled={disabled || !a.submitted_count} onClick={onExport}>匯出此問卷作答</Button><Button className="action destructive small" disabled={disabled} onClick={() => setDeleteOpen(true)}>刪除本次發布</Button></div><small className="survey-muted">{a.definition.items.length} 題 · 版本 {a.scale_version}</small>
    {deleteOpen && <DeleteSurveyDialog assignmentId={a.id} onClose={() => setDeleteOpen(false)} onDeleted={() => { setDeleteOpen(false); onDeleted(); }}/>}
  </article>;
}

修改後

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { NativeSelect, NativeSelectOption } from '@/components/ui/native-select';
import { SurveyAssignDialog } from '@/components/survey-assign-dialog';
import { DeleteSurveyDialog } from '@/components/delete-survey-dialog';
import { rpc } from '@/lib/supabase';
import { appUrl } from '@/lib/routes';
import { shortSurveyPath } from '@/lib/survey-short-link';
import { downloadSurveyQr } from '@/lib/survey-qr';
import { dimensionLabel } from '@/lib/survey-analysis';
import { downloadSurveyCsv, formatSurveyTime, surveyResponsesCsv, taipeiInput, taipeiToUtc, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveyDefinition, type SurveyExport, type SurveyInstrument, type SurveyScores, type SurveyStatus } from '@/lib/surveys';
import '@/app/surveys.css';
import { requireSurveySchema } from '@/lib/survey-schema';

type Workshop = { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null };
type Data = { assignments: SurveyAssignment[]; students: { id: string; name: string }[]; responses: { assignment_id: string; student_id: string; submitted_at: string; scores: SurveyScores }[] };
const answerUrl = (id: string) => appUrl(shortSurveyPath(id));
const itemScoringLabel = (definition: SurveyDefinition, index: number) => definition.scoringStatus === 'unscored' ? '原始回覆，不計分' : definition.scoringStatus === 'pending' ? `${definition.negativeItems?.includes(index + 1) ? '負向敘述；' : ''}計分規則待確認` : definition.reverseItems?.includes(index + 1) ? '反向計分' : '正向計分';

export default function TeacherSurveys({ currentWorkshopId }: { currentWorkshopId?: string }) {
  const [workshops, setWorkshops] = useState<Workshop[]>([]), [selected, setSelected] = useState('');
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<SurveyInstrument[]>([]), [libraryLoading, setLibraryLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false), [error, setError] = useState(''), [message, setMessage] = useState(''), [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);
  const [view, setView] = useState<'assignments' | 'library'>('library'), [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [expandedWorkshop, setExpandedWorkshop] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [assigningWorkshop, setAssigningWorkshop] = useState<Workshop | null>(null);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState<{ instrument: SurveyInstrument; requestId: string } | null>(null);
  const workshop = workshops.find(w => w.id === selected);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController();
    rpc<{ workshops: Workshop[] }>('nptc_analysis_data', {}, abort.signal).then(result => {
      if (abort.signal.aborted) return;
      setWorkshops(result.workshops);
      setSelected(current => result.workshops.some(w => w.id === current) ? current : result.workshops.some(w => w.id === currentWorkshopId) ? currentWorkshopId! : result.workshops.find(w => !w.archived_at)?.id ?? '');
    }).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [currentWorkshopId, revision]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setLibraryLoading(true);
    requireSurveySchema(abort.signal).then(() => rpc<{ instruments: SurveyInstrument[] }>('nptc_survey_library', {}, abort.signal)).then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setLibrary(result.instruments); })
      .catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); })
      .finally(() => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setLibraryLoading(false); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [revision]);
  async function write(name: string, body: Record<string, unknown>, notice = '設定已儲存。') {
    setBusy(true); setError(''); setMessage('');
    try { await rpc(name, { body }); setMessage(notice); setRevision(v => v + 1); return true; }
    catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); return false; }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  async function share(id: string, native = false) {
    setError('');
    try {
      if (native && navigator.share) await navigator.share({ title: '學員問卷填寫', url: answerUrl(id) });
      else { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(answerUrl(id)); setMessage('問卷連結已複製，可貼到課程群組或訊息中傳送。學員登入後即可填寫這份問卷。'); }
    } catch (cause) { if ((cause as Error).name !== 'AbortError') setError('連結未複製；可選取問卷卡片中的網址手動複製。'); }
  }
  async function exportData(workshopId: string, id?: string) {
    setBusy(true); setError('');
    try {
      const result = await rpc<SurveyExport>('nptc_survey_export', { requested_workshop: workshopId });
      if (id) result.responses = result.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === id);
      downloadSurveyCsv(surveyResponsesCsv(result), `NPTC_量表作答_${id ?? workshopId}.csv`);
      setMessage('研究資料已匯出，包含問卷編號、教師備註、原答與分數；不含姓名、Email 或手機。');
    } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <div className="survey-admin">
    <header className="teacher-heading"><div><span className="section-label">SURVEY MANAGEMENT</span><h1>量表管理庫</h1><p>在量表庫查看內容，於發布管理依 OSCE 梯次管理問卷。</p></div></header>
    {error && <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setError(''); setRevision(v => v + 1); }}>重新讀取</Button></p>}
    {message && <p className="notice success" role="status">{message}</p>}
    <nav className="survey-view-tabs" aria-label="量表管理庫分頁"><button disabled={busy} aria-current={view === 'library' ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setView('library')}>量表庫</button><button disabled={busy} aria-current={view === 'assignments' ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => setView('assignments')}>發布管理</button></nav>
    {view === 'library' && <section aria-label="量表庫">
      <div className="survey-library-heading"><div><h2>量表庫</h2><p className="survey-muted">展開查看完整題目，選擇量表後再指定加入的 OSCE 梯次。</p></div><label>搜尋量表<input type="search" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="輸入量表名稱" /></label></div>
      {libraryLoading && <p role="status">正在載入量表庫…</p>}
      {!libraryLoading && library.length === 0 && <p className="empty-panel">量表庫目前尚無可使用的量表。</p>}
      <div className="survey-library-list">{library.filter(i => `${i.definition.title} ${i.version}`.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())).map(instrument => <article className="survey-card" key={`${instrument.code}-${instrument.version}`}>
        <div className="survey-library-title"><div><h3>{instrument.definition.title}</h3><p className="survey-muted">{instrument.definition.items.length} 題 · {instrument.definition.items.some(q => q.type === 'text' || q.type === 'choice') ? '單選與文字回覆' : `${instrument.definition.minScore ?? 1}–${instrument.definition.maxScore ?? 6} 分`} · {instrument.version}</p></div><Button className="action" disabled={busy || !workshops.some(w => !w.archived_at)} onClick={() => { if (workshop?.archived_at) setSelected(workshops.find(w => !w.archived_at)?.id ?? ''); setAdding({ instrument, requestId: crypto.randomUUID() }); }}>加入梯次</Button></div>
        {instrument.definition.notice && <p className="survey-warning">{instrument.definition.notice}</p>}
        <details className="survey-library-content"><summary>展開量表內容</summary>{instrument.definition.instructions.map(p => <p key={p}>{p}</p>)}{!!Object.keys(instrument.definition.dimensions ?? {}).length && <p className="survey-muted">構面：{Object.entries(instrument.definition.dimensions ?? {}).map(([name, items]) => `${dimensionLabel(name)}（${items.length} 題）`).join('、')}</p>}<ol>{instrument.definition.items.map((q, i) => <li key={i}>{q.section && <h4>{q.section}</h4>}{q.text ? <p>{q.text}</p> : <div className="survey-statements"><p><span>左側敘述</span>{q.left}</p><p><span>右側敘述</span>{q.right}</p></div>}{q.options && <p className="survey-muted">選項：{q.options.join('／')}</p>}{q.type === 'text' && <p className="survey-muted">文字意見，{q.required === false ? '選填' : '必填'}</p>}<small className="survey-muted">{itemScoringLabel(instrument.definition,i)}</small></li>)}</ol></details>
        {adding?.instrument === instrument && <form className="survey-add-form" onSubmit={async event => {
          event.preventDefault(); const fields = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
          if (!selected || workshop?.archived_at) { setError('請選擇可發布的 OSCE 梯次。'); return; }
          if (await write('nptc_survey_assign', { workshopId: selected, scaleCode: instrument.code, scaleVersion: instrument.version, requestId: adding.requestId, note: String(fields.get('note') ?? ''), status: fields.get('publish') ? 'open' : 'draft' }, '量表已加入 OSCE 梯次，可在發布管理中複製填答連結。')) { setAdding(null); setExpandedWorkshop(selected); setView('assignments'); }
        }}><label>加入 OSCE 梯次<NativeSelect required aria-label="加入量表的 OSCE 梯次" value={workshop?.archived_at ? '' : selected} disabled={busy} onChange={e => setSelected(e.target.value)}><NativeSelectOption value="">請選擇梯次</NativeSelectOption>{workshops.filter(w => !w.archived_at).map(w => <NativeSelectOption key={w.id} value={w.id}>{w.name}</NativeSelectOption>)}</NativeSelect></label><label>備註（僅老師可見）<textarea name="note" maxLength={2000} rows={2} placeholder="自行註記用途、日期或施測安排" /></label><label className="survey-check-label"><input name="publish" type="checkbox" defaultChecked />加入後立即開放填答</label><div className="survey-export-actions"><Button className="action" type="submit" disabled={busy}>確認加入量表</Button><Button className="action secondary" type="button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setAdding(null)}>取消</Button></div></form>}
      </article>)}</div>
    </section>}
    {view === 'assignments' && <section aria-label="發布管理"><div className="survey-library-heading"><div><h2>發布管理</h2><p className="survey-muted">點選 OSCE 梯次，展開該梯次的問卷、填答連結及完成紀錄。</p></div><Button className="action secondary small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setRevision(v => v + 1)}>更新發布紀錄</Button></div>
      {!workshops.length && <p className="empty-panel">目前沒有 OSCE 梯次，請先建立梯次與學員名冊。</p>}
      <div className="survey-workshop-list">{workshops.map(w => <article className={`survey-workshop-group${expandedWorkshop === w.id ? ' expanded' : ''}`} key={w.id}>
        <button className="survey-workshop-toggle" type="button" disabled={busy} aria-expanded={expandedWorkshop === w.id} aria-controls={`survey-publishing-${w.id}`} onClick={() => { setExpandedWorkshop(current => current === w.id ? null : w.id); setSelected(w.id); setAdding(null); }}><span className="survey-workshop-symbol" aria-hidden="true">▤</span><span className="survey-workshop-copy"><strong>{w.name}</strong><small>{w.archived_at ? '已封存，可查看問卷與回收紀錄' : '查看此梯次的問卷、填答連結與完成紀錄'}</small></span><span className="survey-workshop-open-label">{expandedWorkshop === w.id ? '收合內容' : '查看發布內容'}<span className="survey-workshop-arrow" aria-hidden="true">{expandedWorkshop === w.id ? '⌃' : '⌄'}</span></span></button>
        {expandedWorkshop === w.id && <div id={`survey-publishing-${w.id}`} className="survey-workshop-content"><WorkshopPublishing workshop={w} revision={revision} busy={busy} onAdd={() => { setError(''); setAssigningWorkshop(w); }} onWrite={write} onShare={share} onExport={id => exportData(w.id, id)} onDeleted={() => { setMessage('本次問卷發布已刪除。'); setRevision(v => v + 1); }} /></div>}
      </article>)}</div>
    </section>}
    {assigningWorkshop && <SurveyAssignDialog workshop={assigningWorkshop} instruments={library} busy={busy} error={error} onClose={() => setAssigningWorkshop(null)} onSave={body => write('nptc_survey_assign', body, '量表已加入本梯次，發布內容已更新。')}/>}
  </div>;
}

function WorkshopPublishing({ workshop, revision, busy, onAdd, onWrite, onShare, onExport, onDeleted }: { workshop: Workshop; revision: number; busy: boolean; onAdd: () => void; onWrite: (name: string, body: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<boolean>; onShare: (id: string, native?: boolean) => void; onExport: (id?: string) => void; onDeleted: () => void }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<Data | null>(null), [error, setError] = useState(''), [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    const abort = new AbortController(); setData(null); setError('');
    rpc<Data>('nptc_survey_teacher_data', { requested_workshop: workshop.id }, abort.signal).then(result => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setData(result); }).catch(cause => { if (!abort.signal.aborted) setError(cause.message); });
    return () => abort.abort();
  }, [workshop.id, revision, retry]);
  return <>
    {workshop.archived_at ? <p className="notice">此梯次已封存，僅供檢視及研究匯出。</p> : <Button className="action" disabled={busy} onClick={onAdd}>＋ 加入量表</Button>}
    {error ? <p className="notice error" role="alert">{error} <Button className="action secondary small" onClick={() => setRetry(v => v + 1)}>重新讀取</Button></p> : !data ? <p role="status">正在讀取本梯次發布內容…</p> : !data.assignments.length ? <p className="survey-empty survey-muted">本梯次尚未加入量表，請點選「加入量表」。</p> : <>
      <div className="survey-cards">{data.assignments.map(a => <AssignmentSettings key={`${a.id}-${a.revision}`} assignment={a} count={data.students.length} disabled={busy || !!workshop.archived_at} onSave={body => onWrite('nptc_survey_update', body)} onCopy={() => onShare(a.id)} onShare={() => onShare(a.id, true)} onExport={() => onExport(a.id)} onDeleted={onDeleted} />)}</div>
      <section className="data-panel survey-results"><div className="panel-heading"><div><h2>問卷完成情形</h2><p className="form-help">學員端只顯示填寫狀態；老師可至學習分析儀表板查詢分數與歷次趨勢。</p></div></div><div className="survey-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>學員</th>{data.assignments.map(a => <th key={a.id}>{a.definition.title}<small className="survey-muted">{a.note || '未填備註'}</small></th>)}</tr></thead><tbody>{data.students.map(s => <tr key={s.id}><td>{s.name}</td>{data.assignments.map(a => { const r = data.responses.find(r => r.student_id === s.id && r.assignment_id === a.id); return <td key={a.id}>{r ? <><strong>已完成</strong><small className="survey-muted">{formatSurveyTime(r.submitted_at)}</small></> : <span className="survey-muted">未完成</span>}</td>; })}</tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!data.students.length && <p className="survey-muted">本梯次尚無學員，請先建立名冊。</p>}<div className="survey-export-actions"><Button className="action" disabled={busy || !data.responses.length} onClick={() => onExport()}>匯出本梯次全部問卷作答</Button></div></section>
    </>}
  </>;
}

function AssignmentSettings({ assignment: a, count, disabled, onSave, onCopy, onShare, onExport, onDeleted }: { assignment: SurveyAssignment; count: number; disabled: boolean; onSave: (body: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<boolean>; onCopy: () => void; onShare: () => void; onExport: () => void; onDeleted: () => void }) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<SurveyStatus>(a.status === 'draft' ? 'open' : a.status), [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [deleteOpen, setDeleteOpen] = useState(false);
  const [qrBusy, setQrBusy] = useState(false);
  async function downloadQr() {
    setQrBusy(true); setError('');
    try { await downloadSurveyQr(answerUrl(a.id), a.scale_code, a.id); }
    catch { setError('QR code 下載失敗，請再試一次。'); }
    finally { setQrBusy(false); }
  }
  const linkRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  return <article className="survey-card"><div className="survey-card-heading"><span className="survey-badge">{a.status === 'draft' ? '草稿' : a.status === 'open' ? '已發布' : '已關閉'}</span><span className="survey-muted">已完成 {a.submitted_count ?? 0} / {count} 人</span></div><h2>{a.definition.title}</h2>
    <div className="survey-link-box"><label>學員填答短網址<input ref={linkRef} readOnly value={answerUrl(a.id)} onFocus={e => e.currentTarget.select()} /></label><div className="survey-export-actions"><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" onClick={onCopy}>複製短網址</Button><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" onClick={onShare}>分享連結</Button><Button type="button" className="action secondary small" disabled={qrBusy} onClick={downloadQr}>{qrBusy ? '正在產生 QR code…' : '下載 QR code'}</Button><a className="text-link" href={answerUrl(a.id)} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">開啟問卷 ↗</a></div></div>
    <form onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); setError(''); const fields = new FormData(event.currentTarget); try { const opensAt = taipeiToUtc(String(fields.get('opensAt') ?? '')), closesAt = taipeiToUtc(String(fields.get('closesAt') ?? '')); if (opensAt && closesAt && opensAt >= closesAt) throw new Error('截止時間須晚於開放時間。'); void onSave({ id: a.id, revision: a.revision, status, note: String(fields.get('note') ?? ''), opensAt, closesAt }); } catch (cause) { setError((cause as Error).message); } }}>
      <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="survey-settings"><label>備註（僅老師可見）<textarea name="note" maxLength={2000} rows={2} defaultValue={a.note} /></label><label>發布狀態<NativeSelect value={status} onChange={e => setStatus(e.target.value as SurveyStatus)}><NativeSelectOption value="open">開放填答</NativeSelectOption><NativeSelectOption value="closed">關閉填答</NativeSelectOption></NativeSelect></label><label>開放時間（臺灣時間，可留空）<input type="datetime-local" name="opensAt" defaultValue={taipeiInput(a.opens_at)} /></label><label>截止時間（臺灣時間，可留空）<input type="datetime-local" name="closesAt" defaultValue={taipeiInput(a.closes_at)} /></label><Button className="action" type="submit">{a.status === 'draft' ? '儲存並發布' : '儲存設定'}</Button></fieldset>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="notice error">{error}</p>}
    </form><div className="survey-assignment-footer"><Button className="action secondary small" disabled={disabled || !a.submitted_count} onClick={onExport}>匯出此問卷作答</Button><Button className="action destructive small" disabled={disabled} onClick={() => setDeleteOpen(true)}>刪除本次發布</Button></div><small className="survey-muted">{a.definition.items.length} 題 · 版本 {a.scale_version}</small>
    {deleteOpen && <DeleteSurveyDialog assignmentId={a.id} onClose={() => setDeleteOpen(false)} onDeleted={() => { setDeleteOpen(false); onDeleted(); }}/>}
  </article>;
}
docs/key-renewal.md

# 公開金鑰到期檢查與更新

`pnpm check:key` 只輸出 key 類型、到期時間與剩餘天數，不印出 key。每日 GitHub Pages workflow 及每次部署都會檢查：距到期 90 天內產生 warning，不足 7 天或已過期會停止發布。publishable key 不套用 JWT 的 exp 檢查；service_role 一律拒絕。

本次讀取的公開 anon JWT 到期時間為臺灣時間 **2027-01-10 07:00**。目前仍有效；這次加入檢查與更新流程，沒有直接改動共享 JWT_SECRET 或替換正式機密。

更新時先盤點同一 Supabase 專案的 Kong API key、Auth／PostgREST／Realtime／Storage／Edge 所需設定，區分「API key 字串」與「JWT 簽章設定」。不要只替換前端，也不要把 service_role 放入網站環境變數。

1. 先保留目前設定的安全備份，於隔離環境確認新設定可用。若保持 JWT_SECRET 不變，確認所有會使用 API key 的服務同步採用新字串；若更換 JWT_SECRET，另外評估既有登入 session 與所有服務驗證的影響。
2. 更新伺服器端設定；機密只保留於伺服器／受保護的設定存放處。各服務重啟後確認 API 與 Auth 正常。
3. 更新本機 `.env.local`、Zeabur 前端 `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` 及 GitHub Actions 同名 secret；只放 anon／publishable。
4. 執行 `pnpm check:key`、`pnpm check:api`、`pnpm check` 與正式建置。API 檢查必須讀取錯誤 code；401 本身不能代表權限正常。
5. 部署後核對兩個前端公開資產的角色與期限，再用測試帳號驗證登入、問卷讀取、繳交與管理員資料讀取。錯誤時依備份回復一致設定。

不把任何完整 key、JWT_SECRET、密碼貼進 Git、文件或檢查紀錄。`meta.docx` 已精確排除；提交前可再執行 `node scripts/check-secrets.mjs --staged`。
docs/review-hardening.md

# 程式檢視修正與部署

本次依使用者選定清單實作，首次啟用仍沿用姓名／Email／手機核對，不加入新的身分核對政策。既有量表版本、原始作答與計分規則不改寫。

## 資料庫

既有量表環境先確認版本 2026102905，再執行完整 `supabase/upgrade-review-hardening.sql`。新版為 **2026103001**，可重跑，不搬移資料。新增的刪除影響查詢與稽核只給系統管理者；一般老師的量表摘要與逐題查詢仍受梯次權限限制。

整梯次永久刪除必須重新讀取學員、OSCE 評分、問卷發布及作答筆數，確認完整範圍、輸入梯次名稱、重新驗證目前系統管理者密碼。伺服器鎖住梯次並核對資料指紋後才刪除；稽核只保留操作者、時間、刪除範圍，不保留被刪除的原始答案。

量表頁會檢查此版本；未升級時顯示明確訊息，OSCE 基礎功能的最低版本仍為 2026100701。GitHub 前端發布不會自行執行 SQL。

## 前端與 Edge

- 密碼重設／重新確認信箱保留有效問卷 UUID；短網址轉為同站可驗證的問卷路徑，拒絕外站回跳。
- 分析預設目前梯次，僅下載摘要與分數，點選「查看原答」或分數才讀逐題內容；「所有可檢視梯次」仍可明確選取。
- 未送出問卷離開時提醒；不將真實作答寫入瀏覽器持久儲存。瀏覽器對返回手勢／手機強制關閉的提醒能力有限。
- Edge `student-activate` 以串流限制 4096 位元組，含無 Content-Length 的請求；過大回 413，未通過時不呼叫名冊 RPC。伺服器函式須另部署，前端 GitHub 推送不等於 Edge 已更新。

## 安全標頭

根目錄 `_headers` 與產物的 `_headers` 使用同一份規則：nosniff、Referrer-Policy、禁止嵌入、HSTS 與 CSP。Zeabur 的靜態主機應讀取根目錄規則；需以正式 HTTP 回應確認，不能僅檢查檔案存在。官方說明：[Zeabur 靜態網站](https://zeabur.com/docs/en-US/guides/static)。

GitHub Pages 不提供專案自訂 HTTP 標頭，因此 HTML 同時加入可生效的 CSP 與 referrer meta；frame-ancestors／X-Frame-Options／nosniff 仍須主機支援，不能宣稱 meta 可以取代。CSP 僅允許本站資產及本專案 Supabase 連線；日後更換 API 網域須同步調整 `_headers`。

## 維護檢查

公開金鑰檢查與更新流程見 `key-renewal.md`。`pnpm check` 含機密掃描、型別與回歸測試；`pnpm check:api` 會區分 JWT／金鑰錯誤、缺 RPC、網路失敗及預期權限拒絕。部署後再核對 SQL 版本、正式標頭與問卷入口。
docs/reviews/2026-10-30-review-hardening-validation.md

# 程式檢視修正驗證

## 已完成

- Vite 8.1.6、source-map-js 1.2.2；完整依賴 audit 為 0 則公告。
- 精確排除 meta.docx；追蹤／暫存提交機密掃描不輸出秘密值。
- 公開 key 每次發布與既有每日 workflow 檢查期限，90 天內 warning，不足 7 天拒絕發布；金鑰本身未替換，更新指引在 docs/key-renewal.md。
- 密碼重設與確認信保留有效問卷識別，只回到本站已知路徑。
- 整梯次刪除列出完整影響，伺服器比對指紋、重新驗證管理員並留下刪除事件。舊基礎升級在新版已安裝時拒絕重跑，避免覆蓋保護。
- API 診斷分開判讀權限、JWT、缺 RPC、網路與設定錯誤；已以正式匿名請求確認 42501。
- 量表功能獨立檢查 2026103001；README 更新現況與升級順序。
- 量表分析依梯次讀摘要，原答按需讀取；未繳交問卷加入離開提醒。
- 啟用函式來源加入 4096 位元組串流限制及 413 回應；本機測試涵蓋宣告大小、無大小宣告的串流及 UTF-8 多位元組文字。
- 管理員暫時驗證 client 使用獨立 storage key、finally 清理與請求逾時。

## 驗證

本機使用 Node 24.19.0：27 個測試檔、95 項測試零失敗，型別檢查、正式建置通過。新增 SQL 在 PGlite 的隔離資料庫驗證權限、作答保留、相同筆數但名冊內容改變的過期確認拒絕、永久刪除影響及稽核留存。沒有刪除正式梯次或真實問卷。

正式 SQL 已套用 2026103001，升級前後所有既有名冊、OSCE 評分、量表發布、作答、量表定義雜湊一致；學員帳號與 Auth 帳號數一致。新增四個讀取 RPC 的匿名呼叫均回 42501 權限拒絕。

## 尚未完成的正式操作

瀏覽器工具在原生視窗檢查時無法可靠辨識網址，安全檢查停止了本輪 UI 操作；沒有改用其他自動化方式繞過。

- 正式 `student-activate` 尚未替換為本次來源。正式過大合成請求仍回舊版 400，尚未看到新版 413；GitHub 推送不會自動更新此自架函式。
- 根目錄及產物 `_headers` 已備妥，正式回應是否套用須在前端發布後核對；GitHub Pages 不支援的 HTTP 標頭不能由 HTML meta 取代。
- 未完成真人學員重設密碼、正式問卷繳交、離開提醒與後台導覽的瀏覽器操作驗收。單元／SQL 測試通過不等於以上端到端驗收。

正式發布、HTTP 標頭與 CI 結果另存本機 outputs/verification，不將真實帳密、作答或機密文件推送 GitHub。
docs/surveys.md

修改前

# NPTC 量表庫與獨立問卷連結

2026-10-29 已完成量表功能本機驗證、正式資料庫四段升級的唯讀核對，以及 API 簽章與前端公開金鑰同步。本次依使用者授權合併至 `main`，由既有正式網站流程部署。既有功能驗證見 [驗證結果](reviews/2026-10-29-surveys-final-validation.md)，三次示範施測與發布範圍見 [Demo 交付紀錄](reviews/2026-10-29-survey-demo-release.md)。

## 教師操作

1. 執行 `pnpm dev:demo` 後，開啟本機 `/teacher/`，以 `teacher`／`demo1234` 登入。
2. 進入「量表管理庫」，點選「發布管理」後選擇 OSCE 梯次。
3. 在梯次卡片點選「加入量表」，直接由彈出清單選擇，不會切換分頁。量表庫分頁可另行展開完整題目及說明。
4. 從清單選取量表、填寫備註，再選擇立即開放或先保存草稿。
5. 每次加入均建立獨立問卷及短網址；可按「複製短網址」、「分享連結」或「下載 QR code」。QR code 直接下載為 PNG，不展開圖片。再自行貼到課程群組或訊息中，系統不會自動代寄。
6. 教師可修改發布狀態、開放／截止時間及備註。所有時間欄位以臺灣時間輸入，資料庫以 UTC 儲存。
7. 在「問卷完成情形」查看學員是否繳交；如需研究資料，可按問卷或整個梯次匯出 CSV。

備註已依使用者確認設定為「僅老師可見」。備註不包含在學員公告、問卷頁或分享網址中。量表本身的「量表註記」是另一個欄位，會顯示給學員，例如翻譯初稿及版本適用範圍。

同一份量表可加入不同梯次，也可在同一梯次加入多次。每次加入均獨立計算完成狀態。系統不強制分前後測，也不限制哪份須先完成；需要的分類由教師自行備註。

## 量表內容維護（依最新要求暫不開放新增）

量表庫目前有 DTI-SK、使用者提供的 VPIRS-E 繁體中文建議版，以及依使用者 Excel 欄位建立的學習滿意度問卷。兩份新問卷只加入量表庫，不自動發布至任何梯次。

依使用者最新要求，已移除「新增量表至庫」及「建立修訂版」操作，網站先只供查看及選用既有量表。其他量表日後再由使用者安排加入。資料格式保留以下支援：

- 單一敘述＋數字單選。
- 左右兩個敘述＋數字單選。
- 可設定量尺上下限，範圍為 0–10 的整數。
- 量尺兩端文字、反向題及構面名稱。

本次另支援文字意見、文字選項單選及選填題。尚未提供多選、跳題或逐題不同數字量尺。

加入梯次時會保存完整題目快照；日後新增其他版本時，也不會改變已發布問卷或既有作答。可計分量表依問卷快照由伺服器換算，前端不能指定總分；待確認或不計分問卷只保存原答，總分為 null，不當作 0 分。

DTI-SK 使用先前確認的 14 題，保留左右敘述及 1–6 量尺，第 1、5、6、7、10 題使用修訂文字。持續標示「繁體中文翻譯初稿，未經驗證」。其既定反向題及構面分類仍保存於該版本。

## 學員流程

教師分享的新連結格式為 `/q/?s=22碼代碼`，原 `/student/?survey=問卷發布編號` 繼續有效。短代碼保留完整問卷識別，不需要資料庫對照表，也不包含帳密或學員身分。學員未登入時先輸入帳密，登入後接續開啟該份問卷；已登入且符合梯次名冊時直接填答。

本機示範學員：`student-demo@example.test`／`demo1234`。本次將示範登入改為 Email 與密碼，讓試用方式與正式帳密登入相近；正式環境仍沿用既有 StudentLogin 及帳號啟用流程。

單選題不預選答案；文字題依問卷設定顯示必填或選填。繳交後只顯示已完成及收件時間，不回傳學員原答或分數。網路逾時時保留相同提交編號，可重試原作答，避免建立重複紀錄。

「我的 OSCE 成績」頁僅顯示「問卷填寫公告」，列出量表名稱、是否完成、發布與完成時間。題目不直接放在成績頁，未完成且開放中的問卷可由連結前往填寫。公告 API 不含問卷分數、教師備註、題目、反向規則或其他學員資料。原有 OSCE 成績功能不變。

## 本機資料與正式資料分開

`pnpm dev:demo` 使用瀏覽器 localStorage，僅供同一瀏覽器的本機試用。示範連結中的 localhost 網址不能直接讓其他人的裝置連到您的電腦；正式網站與中央資料庫安裝完成後，才可分享正式網址。

本機舊示範資料會保留。原前後測分類移成可編輯的教師備註，不再用來限制填答順序。這是資料格式調整，不代表舊紀錄被刪除或重新計分。

## 正式升級安排與目前狀態

本專案正式資料庫已核對完成四段量表升級，無須為本次前端發布重裝。以下保留給尚未安裝的新環境；GitHub 建置不會自行執行 SQL。

1. 先依主 README 備份資料並完成既有 NPTC 資料庫。
2. 安裝完整 `supabase/upgrade-surveys.sql`。
3. 接著執行完整 `supabase/upgrade-survey-library.sql`。此檔新增量表庫、獨立問卷加入及問卷連結 RPC，移除固定類別限制，保留原始作答與快照。
4. 接著執行 `supabase/upgrade-survey-analysis.sql`，最後執行 `supabase/upgrade-survey-deletion.sql`。
5. 使用 `verify-surveys.sql` 與 `verify-survey-library.sql` 唯讀檢查安裝；完成真實帳號與專用測試梯次驗收後，再部署正式前端。

第二段升級完成後，第一段升級會拒絕單獨重跑，以保護新量表庫流程；之後的重跑使用第二段升級。推送 GitHub 或前端建置不會自動安裝 SQL。示範分數使用下方獨立種子檔，與資料庫安裝分開。

## Demo 示範梯次的三次量表

既有「Demo 示範梯次（20 位學員）」已加入同一版本 DTI-SK 的三次獨立施測，日期為 2026-10-01、10-15、10-29，各有 01–20 號示範學員的 20 份作答，共 60 份。平均總分依序為 48.95、55.95、59.45；每位學員均有三次紀錄，可查看上升、持平或波動的個人走勢。

操作：老師登入 → 學習分析儀表板 → 量表分數與趨勢 → 選擇該 Demo 梯次及 DTI-SK。選擇全部學員查看各次平均，點選示範學員查看個人趨勢，點分數可看逐題作答與反向換算。

三次發布的教師備註均註明模擬資料，狀態為關閉，避免與新的實際填答混合；學員端仍只公告完成情形。這些人工分數僅供操作示範，不能用作學習成效或量表驗證證據，翻譯初稿未經驗證的標註保留。

來源為 `supabase/seed-demo-surveys.sql`，只選取指定梯次、01–20 號名稱及既有已啟用帳號，不建立 Auth 帳號，不改名冊或 OSCE 成績，也不寫入其他梯次。固定發布與提交識別可重跑，既有資料衝突則停止，不覆寫。此檔不納入 `install.sql`，也不會由建置或部署自動執行。本機 `dev:demo` 的瀏覽器資料與此正式資料庫示範梯次分開保存。

資料表仍位於 `nptc_private`，保留 RLS 與無直接資料表權限的設計。老師 API 逐次確認後臺權限；學員 API 確認已啟用帳號及所屬梯次。直接取得其他梯次的問卷編號也不能繳交。

既有封存與永久刪除保護沿用第一段：封存後僅能依角色查看；有作答的學員不能從名冊直接刪除。完整梯次的永久刪除仍需既有管理者重新驗證流程。

## 資料匯出

問卷作答 CSV 包含梯次、學員代碼、獨立問卷編號、量表代碼與版本、教師備註、每題原答及換算、構面及總分。不同問卷題數會補空欄，未填答不會當作零分。姓名、Email、手機與個人背景不包含在研究匯出內；教師仍可和名冊對照，因此不能稱為完全匿名。

不再提供固定前後測配對按鈕；研究者可依問卷編號、備註及學員代碼安排後續分析。

## 修改紀錄與後續驗證

- 完整第二段修改前後對照：`docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-library-before-after.html`。
- 本段變更與待驗項目：`docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-library.md`。
- 第二、三段開發時依使用者要求暫緩驗證；本次已補驗。歷史紀錄保留當時狀態，現況以 [本次驗證紀錄](reviews/2026-10-29-surveys-final-validation.md) 為準。


## 量表管理庫與發布管理

後臺選單已改名為「量表管理庫」，內有「量表庫」與「發布管理」兩分頁。發布管理以可點選整列的 OSCE 梯次卡片呈現，名稱與說明靠左、展開控制靠右，問卷內容在卡片下方展開。

每個梯次的「加入量表」會開啟同頁清單，填寫教師備註並確認即可加入，不切換到量表庫。量表庫本身仍可展開題目與指定加入的梯次；新增量表及建立修訂版入口維持移除。

## 老師查看量表分數與趨勢

進入「學習分析儀表板」，選擇「量表分數與趨勢」。可篩選 OSCE 梯次、量表版本、學員姓名及分數項目；分數項目包含總分與該量表定義的構面。

- 選擇全部學員時，圖表呈現各次發布的平均，並列出每個點的有效分數筆數。
- 選擇或點選單一學員時，圖表呈現該學員的歷次分數。
- 橫軸按發布時間排序，標示教師備註；未填備註時顯示發布次序。實際填寫時間列於學員分數明細。
- 不同量表或不同版本分開比較；草稿不列入應填份數，未填答留空，不當作 0 分，也不連接缺漏的資料點。
- 點選分數可查看該次保存的題目、原始答案及換算得分。學員端的 API 及成績頁仍只提供完成狀態。
- 跨梯次以作答帳號串接，保留歷史帳號識別；同名不直接視為同一人。資料範圍沿用後臺角色，系統管理者可包含封存梯次。

## 刪除單次問卷發布

問卷卡片新增「刪除本次發布」。開啟時讀取最新作答筆數及設定版本。

- 尚無作答：確認後刪除該次發布，原填答連結失效。
- 已有作答或分數：明確警示筆數、要求勾選資料永久刪除確認，再輸入目前管理員的 Email 及密碼。帳號必須是目前登入且已啟用的後臺管理員。
- 正式模式使用獨立、不持久保存的 Auth client 重新登入；密碼只送至既有 Auth 服務，不傳入問卷刪除 RPC，也不存入日誌或瀏覽器儲存。
- 後端重新計算筆數並檢查設定版本、管理員權限，以及兩分鐘內的密碼登入與一次性 session。期間有新作答或設定改動會拒絕刪除，須重新讀取警示。
- 刪除僅影響該次發布與它的作答，量表庫原始內容、其他發布及其他梯次保留。刪除事件只留下問卷識別、量表版本、操作者、時間及筆數，不保留被刪除的答案。
- 封存梯次須先依既有管理流程還原，才能刪除問卷。

本機示範刪除驗證使用 `teacher-demo@example.test`／`demo1234`，僅模擬本瀏覽器資料操作，不能當成正式身分驗證結果。

## 目前正式升級狀態與新環境順序

目前正式 Supabase 已安裝四段量表升級，並已加入 `upgrade-survey-catalog.sql`。新環境依序安裝現行 NPTC 基礎資料庫、`upgrade-surveys.sql`、`upgrade-survey-library.sql`、`upgrade-survey-analysis.sql`、`upgrade-survey-deletion.sql`、`upgrade-survey-catalog.sql`，再完成正式帳號驗收與前端部署。已安裝量表庫的環境不可重跑第一段舊升級；本次新增內容使用最後一份獨立升級，不重裝基礎資料庫。

先前第三段完整來源對照見 `docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-trends-before-after.html`；驗證中修正的登出與排序之完整檔案對照見 `docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-validation-before-after.html`。正式 Auth、真實帳密及正式永久刪除流程仍須以專用測試梯次驗收。

## VPIRS-E 與學習滿意度

VPIRS-E 保留使用者提供的 25 題、五點量尺及四構面（14／4／3／4 題）。第 15 題保留臨床族群調適；第 19–25 題僅註記負向敘述，未設定為反向計分。依使用者決定，正式規則補充前不產生逐題換算、構面分數、總分或趨勢，只提供完成狀態及原始作答。繁中建議版尚未驗證；西班牙文版文獻不代表此中文版已獲驗證。
