學習滿意度以使用者提供的 `1109滿意度調查表.xlsx`「表單回應 1」B1:P1 的 15 個欄位為題目，文字逐欄核對一致，保留原課程 I／II 命名與講師文字。未匯入來源的 38 筆回覆。使用者確認：11 題五點滿意度必填、推薦意願「是／否」必填、3 題文字意見選填（每題最多 2000 字）。本問卷保存原始回覆，不建立合成總分。

不計分與待確認問卷在老師分析頁顯示狀態，可點「查看原答」。研究 CSV 的未計分欄位留白，另列計分狀態，文字內容保留試算表公式注入防護。學生端仍只公告完成狀態。

## 短網址與 QR code

短網址使用 22 碼無損編碼，完整保留問卷 UUID；不截斷識別碼、不建立新權限或對照資料表。無效或不完整代碼顯示連結錯誤，不導向任意外站。GitHub Pages 子路徑沿用既有 appUrl 設定。

靜態 `/q/` 頁面在 JavaScript 執行前即提供「問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC」的 title、description 與 Open Graph 標籤。此版分享卡片使用通用問卷名稱，不將教師備註或學員資訊公開。

下載 PNG 為 1024×1024、四模組留白、M 級容錯，內容與卡片短網址相同。QR 模組僅在點下載時載入，頁面不展開 QR 圖片。來源採 [node-qrcode](https://github.com/soldair/node-qrcode)；已以獨立 jsQR 解碼檢查圖片的實際連結。

修改後

# NPTC 量表庫與獨立問卷連結

2026-10-29 已完成量表功能本機驗證、正式資料庫四段升級的唯讀核對，以及 API 簽章與前端公開金鑰同步。本次依使用者授權合併至 `main`，由既有正式網站流程部署。既有功能驗證見 [驗證結果](reviews/2026-10-29-surveys-final-validation.md)，三次示範施測與發布範圍見 [Demo 交付紀錄](reviews/2026-10-29-survey-demo-release.md)。

## 教師操作

1. 執行 `pnpm dev:demo` 後，開啟本機 `/teacher/`，以 `teacher`／`demo1234` 登入。
2. 進入「量表管理庫」，點選「發布管理」後選擇 OSCE 梯次。
3. 在梯次卡片點選「加入量表」，直接由彈出清單選擇，不會切換分頁。量表庫分頁可另行展開完整題目及說明。
4. 從清單選取量表、填寫備註，再選擇立即開放或先保存草稿。
5. 每次加入均建立獨立問卷及短網址；可按「複製短網址」、「分享連結」或「下載 QR code」。QR code 直接下載為 PNG，不展開圖片。再自行貼到課程群組或訊息中，系統不會自動代寄。
6. 教師可修改發布狀態、開放／截止時間及備註。所有時間欄位以臺灣時間輸入，資料庫以 UTC 儲存。
7. 在「問卷完成情形」查看學員是否繳交；如需研究資料，可按問卷或整個梯次匯出 CSV。

備註已依使用者確認設定為「僅老師可見」。備註不包含在學員公告、問卷頁或分享網址中。量表本身的「量表註記」是另一個欄位，會顯示給學員，例如翻譯初稿及版本適用範圍。

同一份量表可加入不同梯次，也可在同一梯次加入多次。每次加入均獨立計算完成狀態。系統不強制分前後測，也不限制哪份須先完成；需要的分類由教師自行備註。

## 量表內容維護（依最新要求暫不開放新增）

量表庫目前有 DTI-SK、使用者提供的 VPIRS-E 繁體中文建議版，以及依使用者 Excel 欄位建立的學習滿意度問卷。兩份新問卷只加入量表庫，不自動發布至任何梯次。

依使用者最新要求，已移除「新增量表至庫」及「建立修訂版」操作，網站先只供查看及選用既有量表。其他量表日後再由使用者安排加入。資料格式保留以下支援：

- 單一敘述＋數字單選。
- 左右兩個敘述＋數字單選。
- 可設定量尺上下限，範圍為 0–10 的整數。
- 量尺兩端文字、反向題及構面名稱。

本次另支援文字意見、文字選項單選及選填題。尚未提供多選、跳題或逐題不同數字量尺。

加入梯次時會保存完整題目快照；日後新增其他版本時，也不會改變已發布問卷或既有作答。可計分量表依問卷快照由伺服器換算，前端不能指定總分；待確認或不計分問卷只保存原答，總分為 null，不當作 0 分。

DTI-SK 使用先前確認的 14 題，保留左右敘述及 1–6 量尺，第 1、5、6、7、10 題使用修訂文字。持續標示「繁體中文翻譯初稿，未經驗證」。其既定反向題及構面分類仍保存於該版本。

## 學員流程

教師分享的新連結格式為 `/q/?s=22碼代碼`，原 `/student/?survey=問卷發布編號` 繼續有效。短代碼保留完整問卷識別，不需要資料庫對照表，也不包含帳密或學員身分。學員未登入時先輸入帳密，登入後接續開啟該份問卷；已登入且符合梯次名冊時直接填答。

本機示範學員：`student-demo@example.test`／`demo1234`。本次將示範登入改為 Email 與密碼，讓試用方式與正式帳密登入相近；正式環境仍沿用既有 StudentLogin 及帳號啟用流程。

單選題不預選答案；文字題依問卷設定顯示必填或選填。繳交後只顯示已完成及收件時間，不回傳學員原答或分數。網路逾時時保留相同提交編號，可重試原作答，避免建立重複紀錄。

「我的 OSCE 成績」頁僅顯示「問卷填寫公告」，列出量表名稱、是否完成、發布與完成時間。題目不直接放在成績頁，未完成且開放中的問卷可由連結前往填寫。公告 API 不含問卷分數、教師備註、題目、反向規則或其他學員資料。原有 OSCE 成績功能不變。

## 本機資料與正式資料分開

`pnpm dev:demo` 使用瀏覽器 localStorage，僅供同一瀏覽器的本機試用。示範連結中的 localhost 網址不能直接讓其他人的裝置連到您的電腦；正式網站與中央資料庫安裝完成後，才可分享正式網址。

本機舊示範資料會保留。原前後測分類移成可編輯的教師備註，不再用來限制填答順序。這是資料格式調整，不代表舊紀錄被刪除或重新計分。

## 正式升級安排與目前狀態

量表各段升級順序如下。已完成的舊段落不必重裝；既有 2026102905 環境只需本次 2026103001 升級。GitHub 建置不會自行執行 SQL。

1. 先依主 README 備份資料並完成既有 NPTC 資料庫。
2. 安裝完整 `supabase/upgrade-surveys.sql`。
3. 接著執行完整 `supabase/upgrade-survey-library.sql`。此檔新增量表庫、獨立問卷加入及問卷連結 RPC，移除固定類別限制，保留原始作答與快照。
4. 接著執行 `supabase/upgrade-survey-analysis.sql`，最後執行 `supabase/upgrade-survey-deletion.sql`。
5. 執行 `supabase/upgrade-survey-catalog.sql`（VPIRS-E、滿意度與混合題型），再執行 `supabase/upgrade-review-hardening.sql`（版本 2026103001、分批分析與整梯次刪除保護）。
6. 使用 `verify-surveys.sql` 與 `verify-survey-library.sql` 唯讀檢查安裝；完成真實帳號與專用測試梯次驗收後，再部署正式前端。

第二段升級完成後，第一段升級會拒絕單獨重跑，以保護新量表庫流程；之後的重跑使用第二段升級。推送 GitHub 或前端建置不會自動安裝 SQL。示範分數使用下方獨立種子檔，與資料庫安裝分開。

## Demo 示範梯次的三次量表

既有「Demo 示範梯次（20 位學員）」已加入同一版本 DTI-SK 的三次獨立施測，日期為 2026-10-01、10-15、10-29，各有 01–20 號示範學員的 20 份作答，共 60 份。平均總分依序為 48.95、55.95、59.45；每位學員均有三次紀錄，可查看上升、持平或波動的個人走勢。

操作：老師登入 → 學習分析儀表板 → 量表分數與趨勢 → 選擇該 Demo 梯次及 DTI-SK。選擇全部學員查看各次平均，點選示範學員查看個人趨勢，點分數可看逐題作答與反向換算。

三次發布的教師備註均註明模擬資料，狀態為關閉，避免與新的實際填答混合；學員端仍只公告完成情形。這些人工分數僅供操作示範，不能用作學習成效或量表驗證證據，翻譯初稿未經驗證的標註保留。

來源為 `supabase/seed-demo-surveys.sql`，只選取指定梯次、01–20 號名稱及既有已啟用帳號，不建立 Auth 帳號，不改名冊或 OSCE 成績，也不寫入其他梯次。固定發布與提交識別可重跑，既有資料衝突則停止，不覆寫。此檔不納入 `install.sql`，也不會由建置或部署自動執行。本機 `dev:demo` 的瀏覽器資料與此正式資料庫示範梯次分開保存。

資料表仍位於 `nptc_private`，保留 RLS 與無直接資料表權限的設計。老師 API 逐次確認後臺權限；學員 API 確認已啟用帳號及所屬梯次。直接取得其他梯次的問卷編號也不能繳交。

既有封存與永久刪除保護沿用第一段：封存後僅能依角色查看；有作答的學員不能從名冊直接刪除。完整梯次的永久刪除仍需既有管理者重新驗證流程。

## 資料匯出

問卷作答 CSV 包含梯次、學員代碼、獨立問卷編號、量表代碼與版本、教師備註、每題原答及換算、構面及總分。不同問卷題數會補空欄，未填答不會當作零分。姓名、Email、手機與個人背景不包含在研究匯出內；教師仍可和名冊對照，因此不能稱為完全匿名。

不再提供固定前後測配對按鈕；研究者可依問卷編號、備註及學員代碼安排後續分析。

## 修改紀錄與後續驗證

- 完整第二段修改前後對照：`docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-library-before-after.html`。
- 本段變更與待驗項目：`docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-library.md`。
- 第二、三段開發時依使用者要求暫緩驗證；本次已補驗。歷史紀錄保留當時狀態，現況以 [本次驗證紀錄](reviews/2026-10-29-surveys-final-validation.md) 為準。


## 量表管理庫與發布管理

後臺選單已改名為「量表管理庫」，內有「量表庫」與「發布管理」兩分頁。發布管理以可點選整列的 OSCE 梯次卡片呈現，名稱與說明靠左、展開控制靠右，問卷內容在卡片下方展開。

每個梯次的「加入量表」會開啟同頁清單，填寫教師備註並確認即可加入，不切換到量表庫。量表庫本身仍可展開題目與指定加入的梯次；新增量表及建立修訂版入口維持移除。

## 老師查看量表分數與趨勢

進入「學習分析儀表板」，選擇「量表分數與趨勢」。可篩選 OSCE 梯次、量表版本、學員姓名及分數項目；分數項目包含總分與該量表定義的構面。

- 選擇全部學員時，圖表呈現各次發布的平均，並列出每個點的有效分數筆數。
- 選擇或點選單一學員時，圖表呈現該學員的歷次分數。
- 橫軸按發布時間排序，標示教師備註；未填備註時顯示發布次序。實際填寫時間列於學員分數明細。
- 不同量表或不同版本分開比較；草稿不列入應填份數，未填答留空，不當作 0 分，也不連接缺漏的資料點。
- 點選分數可查看該次保存的題目、原始答案及換算得分。學員端的 API 及成績頁仍只提供完成狀態。
- 跨梯次以作答帳號串接，保留歷史帳號識別；同名不直接視為同一人。資料範圍沿用後臺角色，系統管理者可包含封存梯次。

## 刪除單次問卷發布

問卷卡片新增「刪除本次發布」。開啟時讀取最新作答筆數及設定版本。

- 尚無作答：確認後刪除該次發布，原填答連結失效。
- 已有作答或分數：明確警示筆數、要求勾選資料永久刪除確認，再輸入目前管理員的 Email 及密碼。帳號必須是目前登入且已啟用的後臺管理員。
- 正式模式使用獨立、不持久保存的 Auth client 重新登入；密碼只送至既有 Auth 服務，不傳入問卷刪除 RPC，也不存入日誌或瀏覽器儲存。
- 後端重新計算筆數並檢查設定版本、管理員權限，以及兩分鐘內的密碼登入與一次性 session。期間有新作答或設定改動會拒絕刪除，須重新讀取警示。
- 刪除僅影響該次發布與它的作答，量表庫原始內容、其他發布及其他梯次保留。刪除事件只留下問卷識別、量表版本、操作者、時間及筆數，不保留被刪除的答案。
- 封存梯次須先依既有管理流程還原，才能刪除問卷。

本機示範刪除驗證使用 `teacher-demo@example.test`／`demo1234`，僅模擬本瀏覽器資料操作，不能當成正式身分驗證結果。

## 目前正式升級狀態與新環境順序

目前正式 Supabase 已安裝四段量表升級，並已加入 `upgrade-survey-catalog.sql`。新環境依序安裝現行 NPTC 基礎資料庫、`upgrade-surveys.sql`、`upgrade-survey-library.sql`、`upgrade-survey-analysis.sql`、`upgrade-survey-deletion.sql`、`upgrade-survey-catalog.sql`，再完成正式帳號驗收與前端部署。已安裝量表庫的環境不可重跑第一段舊升級；本次新增內容使用最後一份獨立升級，不重裝基礎資料庫。

先前第三段完整來源對照見 `docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-trends-before-after.html`；驗證中修正的登出與排序之完整檔案對照見 `docs/reviews/2026-10-29-survey-validation-before-after.html`。正式 Auth、真實帳密及正式永久刪除流程仍須以專用測試梯次驗收。

## VPIRS-E 與學習滿意度

VPIRS-E 保留使用者提供的 25 題、五點量尺及四構面（14／4／3／4 題）。第 15 題保留臨床族群調適；第 19–25 題僅註記負向敘述，未設定為反向計分。依使用者決定，正式規則補充前不產生逐題換算、構面分數、總分或趨勢，只提供完成狀態及原始作答。繁中建議版尚未驗證；西班牙文版文獻不代表此中文版已獲驗證。

學習滿意度以使用者提供的 `1109滿意度調查表.xlsx`「表單回應 1」B1:P1 的 15 個欄位為題目，文字逐欄核對一致，保留原課程 I／II 命名與講師文字。未匯入來源的 38 筆回覆。使用者確認：11 題五點滿意度必填、推薦意願「是／否」必填、3 題文字意見選填（每題最多 2000 字）。本問卷保存原始回覆，不建立合成總分。

不計分與待確認問卷在老師分析頁顯示狀態，可點「查看原答」。研究 CSV 的未計分欄位留白，另列計分狀態，文字內容保留試算表公式注入防護。學生端仍只公告完成狀態。

## 短網址與 QR code

短網址使用 22 碼無損編碼，完整保留問卷 UUID；不截斷識別碼、不建立新權限或對照資料表。無效或不完整代碼顯示連結錯誤，不導向任意外站。GitHub Pages 子路徑沿用既有 appUrl 設定。

靜態 `/q/` 頁面在 JavaScript 執行前即提供「問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC」的 title、description 與 Open Graph 標籤。此版分享卡片使用通用問卷名稱，不將教師備註或學員資訊公開。

下載 PNG 為 1024×1024、四模組留白、M 級容錯，內容與卡片短網址相同。QR 模組僅在點下載時載入，頁面不展開 QR 圖片。來源採 [node-qrcode](https://github.com/soldair/node-qrcode)；已以獨立 jsQR 解碼檢查圖片的實際連結。
lib/admin-workshop-action.ts

修改前

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabase } from './supabase';

export async function adminWorkshopAction(
  action: 'archive' | 'delete',
  workshop: { id: string; name: string },
  email: string,
  password: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const { data: current, error: currentError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (currentError || !current.user) throw new Error('登入狀態已失效，請重新登入後臺。');
  if (email.trim().toLowerCase() !== current.user.email?.toLowerCase()) {
    throw new Error('請輸入目前登入的系統管理者帳號。');
  }
  const verification = createClient(import.meta.env.SUPABASE_URL, import.meta.env.SUPABASE_KEY, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false },
  });
  const { data, error } = await verification.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.trim(), password });
  if (error || !data.session || data.user.id !== current.user.id) {
    throw new Error('帳號或密碼驗證失敗，請重新確認。');
  }
  const rpcName = action === 'archive' ? 'nptc_set_workshop_archived' : 'nptc_delete_archived_workshop';
  const body = action === 'archive'
    ? { workshopId: workshop.id, archived: true }
    : { workshopId: workshop.id, name: workshop.name };
  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/rpc/${rpcName}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: {
      apikey: import.meta.env.SUPABASE_KEY,
      Authorization: `Bearer ${data.session.access_token}`,
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
    },
    body: JSON.stringify({ body }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error(typeof result.message === 'string' ? result.message : '操作失敗，請稍後再試。');
  }
}

修改後

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { supabase } from './supabase';

export async function adminWorkshopAction(
  action: 'archive' | 'delete',
  workshop: { id: string; name: string },
  email: string,
  password: string,
  deletion?: { fingerprint: string; acknowledgeDataLoss: boolean },
): Promise<void> {
  const { data: current, error: currentError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (currentError || !current.user) throw new Error('登入狀態已失效，請重新登入後臺。');
  if (email.trim().toLowerCase() !== current.user.email?.toLowerCase()) {
    throw new Error('請輸入目前登入的系統管理者帳號。');
  }
  const verification = createClient(import.meta.env.SUPABASE_URL, import.meta.env.SUPABASE_KEY, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false, storageKey: 'nptc-workshop-confirm' },
  });
  try {
    const { data, error } = await verification.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.trim(), password });
    if (error || !data.session || data.user.id !== current.user.id) {
      throw new Error('帳號或密碼驗證失敗，請重新確認。');
    }
    const rpcName = action === 'archive' ? 'nptc_set_workshop_archived' : 'nptc_delete_archived_workshop';
    const body = action === 'archive'
      ? { workshopId: workshop.id, archived: true }
      : { workshopId: workshop.id, name: workshop.name, ...deletion };
    const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/rpc/${rpcName}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        apikey: import.meta.env.SUPABASE_KEY,
        Authorization: `Bearer ${data.session.access_token}`,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({ body }),
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
      throw new Error(typeof result.message === 'string' ? result.message : '操作失敗，請稍後再試。');
    }
  } finally { await verification.auth.signOut({ scope: 'local' }).catch(() => undefined); }
}
lib/demo.ts

修改前

import { domainTotal, validateDomainMax, validateDomainScores, type DomainValues } from './domains';
import { REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION } from './schema';
import { computeThreshold, DEFAULT_STATIONS, type StationDefinition } from './grading';

// 正式模式：所有名冊、題目與成績都由 Supabase 儲存。
export const DEMO_MODE = import.meta.env?.DEV === true && import.meta.env?.MODE === 'demo';

type DemoRole = 'teacher' | 'student';
type DemoUser = { role: DemoRole; username: string; email: string };
type Workshop = { id: string; name: string; published: number; stations_revision?: number; publishedStations?: string[]; created_at: string; stations: StationDefinition[] };
type Student = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id: string;
  workshop_id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  service_kind?: string;
  nursing_years?: number | null;
  hospital?: string | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  exam_specialty?: string | null;
  first_osce?: boolean | null;
  birth_date?: string | null;
  revision: number;
  updated_at: string;
};
type State = { workshops: Workshop[]; students: Student[]; createRequests?: Record<string,{ id: string; name: string }> };

const storageKey = 'nptc-demo-state-v1';
const sessionKey = 'nptc-demo-user-v1';
const accounts: Record<DemoRole, DemoUser & { password: string }> = {
  teacher: { role: 'teacher', username: 'teacher', email: 'teacher-demo@example.test', password: 'demo1234' },
  student: { role: 'student', username: 'student', email: 'student-demo@example.test', password: 'demo1234' },
};

function initialState(): State {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    workshops: [{ id: 'demo-workshop', name: '示範 OSCE 班', published: 0, publishedStations: ['q1','q2','q3','q4'], created_at: now, stations: [
      { key: 'q1', title: '初步評估與處置', testDate: '2026-09-01', complaint: '胸痛', diagnosis: '急性冠心症', prompt: '依個案主訴完成初步評估、臨床推理與處置說明。' },
      { key: 'q2', title: '溝通與衛教', testDate: '2026-09-01', complaint: '胸痛', diagnosis: '急性冠心症', prompt: '以病人可理解的方式說明評估結果與後續處置。' },
      { key: 'q3', title: '病況辨識', testDate: '2026-09-02', complaint: '呼吸困難', diagnosis: '肺炎', prompt: '辨識關鍵臨床線索，提出優先處置與追蹤計畫。' },
      { key: 'q4', title: '整合照護', testDate: '2026-09-02', complaint: '發燒', diagnosis: '敗血症', prompt: '整合病史、檢查與照護需求，完成臨床決策。' },
    ] }],
    students: [{
      id: 'demo-student', workshop_id: 'demo-workshop', name: '示範學員', email: accounts.student.email,
      phone: '0912345678', revision: 0, q1_score: 72, q1_rating: 3, q1_feedback: '評估方向清楚，可再補充鑑別診斷依據。', q2_score: 65, q2_rating: 3, q2_feedback: '',
      q3_score: 78, q3_rating: 4, q4_score: 58, q4_rating: 2, updated_at: now, updated_by: accounts.teacher.email,
    }],
  };
}

function state(): State {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!saved) return initialState();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved) as State;
    parsed.students.forEach((student) => {
      if (!student.phone) student.phone = '0912345678';
    });
    return parsed;
  } catch { return initialState(); }
}
function save(next: State) { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(next)); }
function thresholds(students: Student[], stations: StationDefinition[]) {
  return stations.map(({ key }) => {
    const grades = students.map(student => ({ score: student[`${key}_score`] as number | null, rating: student[`${key}_rating`] as number | null }));
    return { key, ...computeThreshold(grades) };
  });
}

export function demoUser(): DemoUser | null {
  try { const value = localStorage.getItem(sessionKey); return value ? JSON.parse(value) as DemoUser : null; } catch { return null; }
}
export function signInDemo(role: DemoRole, username: string, password: string) {
  const account = accounts[role];
  if ((username !== account.username && !(role === 'student' && username.trim().toLowerCase() === account.email)) || password !== account.password) throw new Error('帳號或密碼不正確。');
  const user: DemoUser = { role: account.role, username: account.username, email: account.email };
  localStorage.setItem(sessionKey, JSON.stringify(user));
}
export function signInDemoStudent(email: string, phone: string) {
  const student = state().students.find((item) => item.email === email.trim().toLowerCase() && item.phone === phone.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, ''));
  if (!student) throw new Error('找不到相符的學員資料，請確認 Email 與手機電話。');
  const user: DemoUser = { role: 'student', username: student.email, email: student.email };
  localStorage.setItem(sessionKey, JSON.stringify(user));
}
export function signOutDemo() { localStorage.removeItem(sessionKey); }

function requireUser() { const user = demoUser(); if (!user) throw new Error('請先登入。'); return user; }
function requireTeacher() { const user = requireUser(); if (user.role !== 'teacher') throw new Error('此帳號沒有老師權限。'); return user; }
async function hospitalDirectory(): Promise<{ hospitals: { name: string; city: string; level: string }[] }> {
  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env?.BASE_URL || '/'}data/accredited-hospitals.json`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('官方醫院名冊暫時無法載入。');
  return response.json();
}

export async function demoRpc<T>(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {
  const user = requireUser();
  if (name === 'nptc_is_teacher') return (user.role === 'teacher') as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_schema_version') return REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_hospital_directory') return await hospitalDirectory() as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_sync_student_email') return { ok: true } as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_is_account_reviewer') return false as T;
  const data = state();
  if (name.startsWith('nptc_survey_')) {
    const { demoSurveyRpc } = await import('./survey-demo');
    return demoSurveyRpc(name, args, { user, workshops: data.workshops, students: data.students }) as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_teacher_data' || name === 'nptc_analysis_data') {
    requireTeacher();
    const requested = args.requested_workshop as string | null | undefined;
    const selected = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === requested) ?? data.workshops[0] ?? null;
    const students = selected ? data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === selected.id).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh-TW')) : [];
    return { includesArchived: false, workshops: data.workshops, selected, students, thresholds: selected ? thresholds(students, selected.stations) : [] } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_student_data') {
    const records = data.students.filter(item => item.email === user.email).map(student => {
      const workshop = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === student.workshop_id)!;
      const visibleStations = workshop.stations.filter((station) => (workshop.publishedStations ?? (workshop.published ? workshop.stations.map((item) => item.key) : [])).includes(station.key));
      const published = visibleStations.length ? 1 : 0;
      return {
        id: student.id, name: student.name, email: student.email, phone: student.phone, workshopName: workshop.name, archived: false, published,
        profile: { serviceKind: student.service_kind ?? 'hospital', nursingYears: student.nursing_years ?? null, hospital: student.hospital ?? '', unit: student.unit ?? '', examSpecialty: student.exam_specialty ?? '', firstOsce: student.first_osce ?? null, birthDate: student.birth_date ?? '' },
        stations: visibleStations,
        updatedAt: published ? student.updated_at : null,
        grades: published ? visibleStations.map(({ key }) => ({ key, score: student[`${key}_score`] ?? null, rating: student[`${key}_rating`] ?? null, feedback: student[`${key}_feedback`] ?? '', domains: student[`${key}_domains`] ?? null })) : [],
        thresholds: published ? thresholds(data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === workshop.id), visibleStations) : [],
      };
    });
    return { records } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores' || name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores_v2' || name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores_v3') {
    const teacher = requireTeacher();
    const body = args.body as { workshopId?: string; station?: string; items?: Array<{ id: string; revision: number; score: number | null; rating: number | null; feedback: string; domains?: DomainValues | null }> };
    const workshop = data.workshops.find((item) => item.id === body.workshopId);
    if (!workshop || workshop.published) throw new Error('請先撤回公布，再修改成績。');
    if (!workshop.stations.some((item) => item.key === body.station) || !body.items?.length) throw new Error('請選擇題目與至少一位學員。');
    const definition=workshop.stations.find(item=>item.key===body.station)!;
    if(workshop.publishedStations?.includes(definition.key))throw new Error('請先撤回本題公告。');
    validateDomainMax(definition.domainMax);
    for (const item of body.items) {
      if(item.domains===undefined)throw new Error('請使用五面向成績表。');
      item.score=item.domains===null ? null : domainTotal(validateDomainScores(item.domains,definition.domainMax));
      const student = data.students.find((value) => value.id === item.id && value.workshop_id === workshop.id);
      if (!student || student.revision !== item.revision) throw new Error('資料已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
      if ((item.score === null) !== (item.rating === null) || (item.score !== null && (!Number.isFinite(item.score) || item.score < 0 || item.score > 100 || !Number.isInteger(item.rating) || item.rating! < 1 || item.rating! > 5))) throw new Error('分數與 Global Rating 請成對填寫，分數為 0–100、Rating 為 1–5。');
      if (item.feedback.length > 500) throw new Error('質性回饋最多 500 字。');
      student[`${body.station}_domains`] = item.domains; student[`${body.station}_score`] = item.score; student[`${body.station}_rating`] = item.rating; student[`${body.station}_feedback`] = item.feedback;
      student.revision += 1; student.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); student.updated_by = teacher.email;
    }
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_student_update_profile') {
    const body = args.body as { serviceKind?: string; nursingYears?: number; hospital?: string; unit?: string; examSpecialty?: string; firstOsce?: boolean; birthDate?: string };
    if (!Number.isInteger(body.nursingYears) || body.nursingYears! < 0 || body.nursingYears! > 60 || !body.hospital?.trim()  || !body.examSpecialty?.trim() || typeof body.firstOsce !== 'boolean' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(body.birthDate ?? '')) throw new Error('請完整填寫個人資料。');
    data.students.filter((student) => student.email === user.email).forEach((student) => Object.assign(student, { service_kind: body.serviceKind ?? 'hospital', nursing_years: body.nursingYears, hospital: body.hospital!.trim(), unit: body.unit!.trim(), exam_specialty: body.examSpecialty!.trim(), first_osce: body.firstOsce, birth_date: body.birthDate }));
    if ((!body.serviceKind || body.serviceKind === 'hospital') && !(await hospitalDirectory()).hospitals.some(h=>h.name===body.hospital)) throw new Error('請從目前的官方醫院名冊選擇服務醫院。');
    if (body.birthDate! > new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) || body.birthDate! < '1900-01-01' || !['內科','精神科','兒科','外科','婦產科','麻醉科','家庭科'].includes(body.examSpecialty!)) throw new Error('請確認出生年月日與科別。');
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (name !== 'nptc_teacher_write' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_save_stations' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_save_stations_v3' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_publish_station') throw new Error('不支援的展示資料操作。');
  const teacher = requireTeacher();
  const body = args.body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const action = name.includes('save_stations') ? 'saveStations' : name === 'nptc_teacher_publish_station' ? 'publishStation' : body.action as string;
  if (action === 'publish' || action === 'saveScores') throw new Error('請使用逐題公布與五面向成績表。');
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  if (action === 'createWorkshop') {
    const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim(); if (!name) throw new Error('請輸入梯次名稱。');
    if (typeof body.requestId !== 'string' || !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(body.requestId)) throw new Error('請重新載入後建立梯次。');
    const previous = data.createRequests?.[body.requestId];
    if (previous) { if(previous.name!==name)throw new Error('建立請求的名稱不同，請重新整理。'); return { id: previous.id } as T; }
    const id = crypto.randomUUID(); data.createRequests ??= {}; data.createRequests[body.requestId] = { id, name }; data.workshops.unshift({ id, name, stations_revision: 0, published: 0, created_at: now, stations: structuredClone(DEFAULT_STATIONS) }); save(data); return { id } as T;
  }
  const workshop = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === body.workshopId);
  if (!workshop) throw new Error('找不到此梯次。');
  const visibleStations = new Set(workshop.publishedStations ?? (workshop.published ? workshop.stations.map(s=>s.key) : []));
  if (['saveStudent','deleteStudent','bulkImportStudents'].includes(action) && visibleStations.size) throw new Error('請先撤回本梯次所有題目公告，再修改名冊。');
  const id = body.id as string | undefined;
  const current = id ? data.students.find(item => item.id === id && item.workshop_id === workshop.id) : undefined;
  if (id && (!current || current.revision !== body.revision)) throw new Error('資料已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
  if (action === 'deleteStudent') { data.students = data.students.filter(item => item !== current); save(data); return { ok: true } as T; }
  if (action === 'saveStations') {
    if (body.stationsRevision !== (workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)) throw new Error('題目設定已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
    const stations = body.stations as StationDefinition[];
    for (const previous of workshop.stations) {
      const next=stations?.find(s=>s.key===previous.key);
      if(visibleStations.has(previous.key) && JSON.stringify(previous)!==JSON.stringify(next)) throw new Error('請先撤回本題公告，再修改題目。');
      if(!next && data.students.some(s=>s.workshop_id===workshop.id && (s[`${previous.key}_score`]!=null || !!s[`${previous.key}_feedback`]))) throw new Error('有成績、回饋或已公告的題目不可刪除。');
    }
    if (new Set(stations?.map(s=>s.key)).size !== stations?.length) throw new Error('題目代碼不可重複。');
    if (!Array.isArray(stations) || !stations.length) throw new Error('請至少新增一題 OSCE 題目。');
    workshop.stations = stations.map((station, index) => { const title = String(station?.title ?? '').trim(), testDate = String(station?.testDate ?? ''), complaint = String(station?.complaint ?? '').trim(), diagnosis = String(station?.diagnosis ?? '').trim(), prompt = String(station?.prompt ?? '').trim(); if (!station?.key || !title || !testDate || !complaint || !diagnosis || !prompt) throw new Error('每題都必須完成題目名稱、測驗日期、個案主訴、最終診斷與命題內容摘要。'); const previous=workshop.stations.find(s=>s.key===station.key); const domainMax=station.domainMax ? validateDomainMax(station.domainMax) : undefined; if(!domainMax && (!previous?.title || previous.domainMax))throw new Error('請填寫五面向滿分。'); if(JSON.stringify(previous?.domainMax)!==JSON.stringify(domainMax) && data.students.some(s=>s.workshop_id===workshop.id && s[`${station.key}_domains`]))throw new Error('已有分項成績，不能變更配分。'); return { key: String(station.key), title, testDate, complaint, diagnosis, prompt, ...(domainMax ? {domainMax} : {}) }; });
    workshop.stations_revision=(workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)+1;
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'publishStation') {
    const station = String(body.station ?? ''); const published = Boolean(body.published);
    if (!workshop.stations.some((item) => item.key === station)) throw new Error('找不到指定題目。');
    if (published && !data.students.some((item) => item.workshop_id === workshop.id && item[`${station}_score`] !== null && item[`${station}_score`] !== undefined)) throw new Error('本題至少要有一筆已登錄成績才能公布。');
    const visible = visibleStations; published ? visible.add(station) : visible.delete(station); workshop.publishedStations = [...visible]; workshop.published=0; workshop.stations_revision=(workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)+1; save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'bulkImportStudents') {
    const rows = body.students as Array<{ name?: string; email?: string; phone?: string }>;
    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) throw new Error('請至少提供一位學員。');
    const existing = data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === workshop.id);
    const seenEmails = new Set(existing.map(item => item.email));
    const seenPhones = new Set(existing.map(item => item.phone));
    const next = rows.map(row => ({ name: String(row.name ?? '').trim(), email: String(row.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(), phone: String(row.phone ?? '').trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '') }));
    for (const row of next) {
      if (!row.name || !row.email || !/^09\d{8}$/.test(row.phone) || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(row.email)) throw new Error('匯入資料需包含有效的姓名、Email 與手機電話。');
      if (seenEmails.has(row.email) || seenPhones.has(row.phone)) throw new Error(`Email 或手機電話重複：${row.email}`);
      seenEmails.add(row.email); seenPhones.add(row.phone);
    }
    data.students.push(...next.map(row => ({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshop.id, ...row, revision: 0, q1_score: null, q1_rating: null, q2_score: null, q2_rating: null, q3_score: null, q3_rating: null, q4_score: null, q4_rating: null, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email })));
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'saveStudent') {
    const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim(), email = String(body.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(), phone = String(body.phone ?? '').trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
    if (!name || !email || !/^09\d{8}$/.test(phone)) throw new Error('請完整填寫有效的姓名、Email 與手機電話。');
    if (data.students.some(item => item !== current && item.workshop_id === workshop.id && (item.email === email || item.phone === phone))) throw new Error('此梯次已有相同的 Email 或手機電話。');
    if (current) { Object.assign(current, { name, email, phone, revision: current.revision + 1, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email }); }
    else data.students.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshop.id, name, email, phone, revision: 0, q1_score: null, q1_rating: null, q2_score: null, q2_rating: null, q3_score: null, q3_rating: null, q4_score: null, q4_rating: null, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email });
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  throw new Error('不支援的操作。');
}

修改後

import { domainTotal, validateDomainMax, validateDomainScores, type DomainValues } from './domains';
import { REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION } from './schema';
import { computeThreshold, DEFAULT_STATIONS, type StationDefinition } from './grading';

// 正式模式：所有名冊、題目與成績都由 Supabase 儲存。
export const DEMO_MODE = import.meta.env?.DEV === true && import.meta.env?.MODE === 'demo';

type DemoRole = 'teacher' | 'student';
type DemoUser = { role: DemoRole; username: string; email: string };
type Workshop = { id: string; name: string; published: number; stations_revision?: number; publishedStations?: string[]; created_at: string; stations: StationDefinition[] };
type Student = Record<string, unknown> & {
  id: string;
  workshop_id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  service_kind?: string;
  nursing_years?: number | null;
  hospital?: string | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  exam_specialty?: string | null;
  first_osce?: boolean | null;
  birth_date?: string | null;
  revision: number;
  updated_at: string;
};
type State = { workshops: Workshop[]; students: Student[]; createRequests?: Record<string,{ id: string; name: string }> };

const storageKey = 'nptc-demo-state-v1';
const sessionKey = 'nptc-demo-user-v1';
const accounts: Record<DemoRole, DemoUser & { password: string }> = {
  teacher: { role: 'teacher', username: 'teacher', email: 'teacher-demo@example.test', password: 'demo1234' },
  student: { role: 'student', username: 'student', email: 'student-demo@example.test', password: 'demo1234' },
};

function initialState(): State {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  return {
    workshops: [{ id: 'demo-workshop', name: '示範 OSCE 班', published: 0, publishedStations: ['q1','q2','q3','q4'], created_at: now, stations: [
      { key: 'q1', title: '初步評估與處置', testDate: '2026-09-01', complaint: '胸痛', diagnosis: '急性冠心症', prompt: '依個案主訴完成初步評估、臨床推理與處置說明。' },
      { key: 'q2', title: '溝通與衛教', testDate: '2026-09-01', complaint: '胸痛', diagnosis: '急性冠心症', prompt: '以病人可理解的方式說明評估結果與後續處置。' },
      { key: 'q3', title: '病況辨識', testDate: '2026-09-02', complaint: '呼吸困難', diagnosis: '肺炎', prompt: '辨識關鍵臨床線索，提出優先處置與追蹤計畫。' },
      { key: 'q4', title: '整合照護', testDate: '2026-09-02', complaint: '發燒', diagnosis: '敗血症', prompt: '整合病史、檢查與照護需求，完成臨床決策。' },
    ] }],
    students: [{
      id: 'demo-student', workshop_id: 'demo-workshop', name: '示範學員', email: accounts.student.email,
      phone: '0912345678', revision: 0, q1_score: 72, q1_rating: 3, q1_feedback: '評估方向清楚，可再補充鑑別診斷依據。', q2_score: 65, q2_rating: 3, q2_feedback: '',
      q3_score: 78, q3_rating: 4, q4_score: 58, q4_rating: 2, updated_at: now, updated_by: accounts.teacher.email,
    }],
  };
}

function state(): State {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!saved) return initialState();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved) as State;
    parsed.students.forEach((student) => {
      if (!student.phone) student.phone = '0912345678';
    });
    return parsed;
  } catch { return initialState(); }
}
function save(next: State) { localStorage.setItem(storageKey, JSON.stringify(next)); }
function thresholds(students: Student[], stations: StationDefinition[]) {
  return stations.map(({ key }) => {
    const grades = students.map(student => ({ score: student[`${key}_score`] as number | null, rating: student[`${key}_rating`] as number | null }));
    return { key, ...computeThreshold(grades) };
  });
}

export function demoUser(): DemoUser | null {
  try { const value = localStorage.getItem(sessionKey); return value ? JSON.parse(value) as DemoUser : null; } catch { return null; }
}
export function signInDemo(role: DemoRole, username: string, password: string) {
  const account = accounts[role];
  if ((username !== account.username && !(role === 'student' && username.trim().toLowerCase() === account.email)) || password !== account.password) throw new Error('帳號或密碼不正確。');
  const user: DemoUser = { role: account.role, username: account.username, email: account.email };
  localStorage.setItem(sessionKey, JSON.stringify(user));
}
export function signInDemoStudent(email: string, phone: string) {
  const student = state().students.find((item) => item.email === email.trim().toLowerCase() && item.phone === phone.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, ''));
  if (!student) throw new Error('找不到相符的學員資料，請確認 Email 與手機電話。');
  const user: DemoUser = { role: 'student', username: student.email, email: student.email };
  localStorage.setItem(sessionKey, JSON.stringify(user));
}
export function signOutDemo() { localStorage.removeItem(sessionKey); }

function requireUser() { const user = demoUser(); if (!user) throw new Error('請先登入。'); return user; }
function requireTeacher() { const user = requireUser(); if (user.role !== 'teacher') throw new Error('此帳號沒有老師權限。'); return user; }
async function hospitalDirectory(): Promise<{ hospitals: { name: string; city: string; level: string }[] }> {
  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env?.BASE_URL || '/'}data/accredited-hospitals.json`);
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error('官方醫院名冊暫時無法載入。');
  return response.json();
}

export async function demoRpc<T>(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<T> {
  const user = requireUser();
  if (name === 'nptc_is_teacher') return (user.role === 'teacher') as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_schema_version') return Math.max(REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION, 2026103001) as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_hospital_directory') return await hospitalDirectory() as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_sync_student_email') return { ok: true } as T;
  if (name === 'nptc_is_account_reviewer') return false as T;
  const data = state();
  if (name.startsWith('nptc_survey_')) {
    const { demoSurveyRpc } = await import('./survey-demo');
    return demoSurveyRpc(name, args, { user, workshops: data.workshops, students: data.students }) as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_teacher_data' || name === 'nptc_analysis_data') {
    requireTeacher();
    const requested = args.requested_workshop as string | null | undefined;
    const selected = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === requested) ?? data.workshops[0] ?? null;
    const students = selected ? data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === selected.id).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh-TW')) : [];
    return { includesArchived: false, workshops: data.workshops, selected, students, thresholds: selected ? thresholds(students, selected.stations) : [] } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_student_data') {
    const records = data.students.filter(item => item.email === user.email).map(student => {
      const workshop = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === student.workshop_id)!;
      const visibleStations = workshop.stations.filter((station) => (workshop.publishedStations ?? (workshop.published ? workshop.stations.map((item) => item.key) : [])).includes(station.key));
      const published = visibleStations.length ? 1 : 0;
      return {
        id: student.id, name: student.name, email: student.email, phone: student.phone, workshopName: workshop.name, archived: false, published,
        profile: { serviceKind: student.service_kind ?? 'hospital', nursingYears: student.nursing_years ?? null, hospital: student.hospital ?? '', unit: student.unit ?? '', examSpecialty: student.exam_specialty ?? '', firstOsce: student.first_osce ?? null, birthDate: student.birth_date ?? '' },
        stations: visibleStations,
        updatedAt: published ? student.updated_at : null,
        grades: published ? visibleStations.map(({ key }) => ({ key, score: student[`${key}_score`] ?? null, rating: student[`${key}_rating`] ?? null, feedback: student[`${key}_feedback`] ?? '', domains: student[`${key}_domains`] ?? null })) : [],
        thresholds: published ? thresholds(data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === workshop.id), visibleStations) : [],
      };
    });
    return { records } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores' || name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores_v2' || name === 'nptc_teacher_batch_scores_v3') {
    const teacher = requireTeacher();
    const body = args.body as { workshopId?: string; station?: string; items?: Array<{ id: string; revision: number; score: number | null; rating: number | null; feedback: string; domains?: DomainValues | null }> };
    const workshop = data.workshops.find((item) => item.id === body.workshopId);
    if (!workshop || workshop.published) throw new Error('請先撤回公布，再修改成績。');
    if (!workshop.stations.some((item) => item.key === body.station) || !body.items?.length) throw new Error('請選擇題目與至少一位學員。');
    const definition=workshop.stations.find(item=>item.key===body.station)!;
    if(workshop.publishedStations?.includes(definition.key))throw new Error('請先撤回本題公告。');
    validateDomainMax(definition.domainMax);
    for (const item of body.items) {
      if(item.domains===undefined)throw new Error('請使用五面向成績表。');
      item.score=item.domains===null ? null : domainTotal(validateDomainScores(item.domains,definition.domainMax));
      const student = data.students.find((value) => value.id === item.id && value.workshop_id === workshop.id);
      if (!student || student.revision !== item.revision) throw new Error('資料已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
      if ((item.score === null) !== (item.rating === null) || (item.score !== null && (!Number.isFinite(item.score) || item.score < 0 || item.score > 100 || !Number.isInteger(item.rating) || item.rating! < 1 || item.rating! > 5))) throw new Error('分數與 Global Rating 請成對填寫，分數為 0–100、Rating 為 1–5。');
      if (item.feedback.length > 500) throw new Error('質性回饋最多 500 字。');
      student[`${body.station}_domains`] = item.domains; student[`${body.station}_score`] = item.score; student[`${body.station}_rating`] = item.rating; student[`${body.station}_feedback`] = item.feedback;
      student.revision += 1; student.updated_at = new Date().toISOString(); student.updated_by = teacher.email;
    }
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_student_update_profile') {
    const body = args.body as { serviceKind?: string; nursingYears?: number; hospital?: string; unit?: string; examSpecialty?: string; firstOsce?: boolean; birthDate?: string };
    if (!Number.isInteger(body.nursingYears) || body.nursingYears! < 0 || body.nursingYears! > 60 || !body.hospital?.trim()  || !body.examSpecialty?.trim() || typeof body.firstOsce !== 'boolean' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(body.birthDate ?? '')) throw new Error('請完整填寫個人資料。');
    data.students.filter((student) => student.email === user.email).forEach((student) => Object.assign(student, { service_kind: body.serviceKind ?? 'hospital', nursing_years: body.nursingYears, hospital: body.hospital!.trim(), unit: body.unit!.trim(), exam_specialty: body.examSpecialty!.trim(), first_osce: body.firstOsce, birth_date: body.birthDate }));
    if ((!body.serviceKind || body.serviceKind === 'hospital') && !(await hospitalDirectory()).hospitals.some(h=>h.name===body.hospital)) throw new Error('請從目前的官方醫院名冊選擇服務醫院。');
    if (body.birthDate! > new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) || body.birthDate! < '1900-01-01' || !['內科','精神科','兒科','外科','婦產科','麻醉科','家庭科'].includes(body.examSpecialty!)) throw new Error('請確認出生年月日與科別。');
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (name !== 'nptc_teacher_write' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_save_stations' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_save_stations_v3' && name !== 'nptc_teacher_publish_station') throw new Error('不支援的展示資料操作。');
  const teacher = requireTeacher();
  const body = args.body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const action = name.includes('save_stations') ? 'saveStations' : name === 'nptc_teacher_publish_station' ? 'publishStation' : body.action as string;
  if (action === 'publish' || action === 'saveScores') throw new Error('請使用逐題公布與五面向成績表。');
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  if (action === 'createWorkshop') {
    const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim(); if (!name) throw new Error('請輸入梯次名稱。');
    if (typeof body.requestId !== 'string' || !/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(body.requestId)) throw new Error('請重新載入後建立梯次。');
    const previous = data.createRequests?.[body.requestId];
    if (previous) { if(previous.name!==name)throw new Error('建立請求的名稱不同，請重新整理。'); return { id: previous.id } as T; }
    const id = crypto.randomUUID(); data.createRequests ??= {}; data.createRequests[body.requestId] = { id, name }; data.workshops.unshift({ id, name, stations_revision: 0, published: 0, created_at: now, stations: structuredClone(DEFAULT_STATIONS) }); save(data); return { id } as T;
  }
  const workshop = data.workshops.find(item => item.id === body.workshopId);
  if (!workshop) throw new Error('找不到此梯次。');
  const visibleStations = new Set(workshop.publishedStations ?? (workshop.published ? workshop.stations.map(s=>s.key) : []));
  if (['saveStudent','deleteStudent','bulkImportStudents'].includes(action) && visibleStations.size) throw new Error('請先撤回本梯次所有題目公告，再修改名冊。');
  const id = body.id as string | undefined;
  const current = id ? data.students.find(item => item.id === id && item.workshop_id === workshop.id) : undefined;
  if (id && (!current || current.revision !== body.revision)) throw new Error('資料已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
  if (action === 'deleteStudent') { data.students = data.students.filter(item => item !== current); save(data); return { ok: true } as T; }
  if (action === 'saveStations') {
    if (body.stationsRevision !== (workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)) throw new Error('題目設定已更新，請重新載入後再編輯。');
    const stations = body.stations as StationDefinition[];
    for (const previous of workshop.stations) {
      const next=stations?.find(s=>s.key===previous.key);
      if(visibleStations.has(previous.key) && JSON.stringify(previous)!==JSON.stringify(next)) throw new Error('請先撤回本題公告，再修改題目。');
      if(!next && data.students.some(s=>s.workshop_id===workshop.id && (s[`${previous.key}_score`]!=null || !!s[`${previous.key}_feedback`]))) throw new Error('有成績、回饋或已公告的題目不可刪除。');
    }
    if (new Set(stations?.map(s=>s.key)).size !== stations?.length) throw new Error('題目代碼不可重複。');
    if (!Array.isArray(stations) || !stations.length) throw new Error('請至少新增一題 OSCE 題目。');
    workshop.stations = stations.map((station, index) => { const title = String(station?.title ?? '').trim(), testDate = String(station?.testDate ?? ''), complaint = String(station?.complaint ?? '').trim(), diagnosis = String(station?.diagnosis ?? '').trim(), prompt = String(station?.prompt ?? '').trim(); if (!station?.key || !title || !testDate || !complaint || !diagnosis || !prompt) throw new Error('每題都必須完成題目名稱、測驗日期、個案主訴、最終診斷與命題內容摘要。'); const previous=workshop.stations.find(s=>s.key===station.key); const domainMax=station.domainMax ? validateDomainMax(station.domainMax) : undefined; if(!domainMax && (!previous?.title || previous.domainMax))throw new Error('請填寫五面向滿分。'); if(JSON.stringify(previous?.domainMax)!==JSON.stringify(domainMax) && data.students.some(s=>s.workshop_id===workshop.id && s[`${station.key}_domains`]))throw new Error('已有分項成績，不能變更配分。'); return { key: String(station.key), title, testDate, complaint, diagnosis, prompt, ...(domainMax ? {domainMax} : {}) }; });
    workshop.stations_revision=(workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)+1;
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'publishStation') {
    const station = String(body.station ?? ''); const published = Boolean(body.published);
    if (!workshop.stations.some((item) => item.key === station)) throw new Error('找不到指定題目。');
    if (published && !data.students.some((item) => item.workshop_id === workshop.id && item[`${station}_score`] !== null && item[`${station}_score`] !== undefined)) throw new Error('本題至少要有一筆已登錄成績才能公布。');
    const visible = visibleStations; published ? visible.add(station) : visible.delete(station); workshop.publishedStations = [...visible]; workshop.published=0; workshop.stations_revision=(workshop.stations_revision ?? 0)+1; save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'bulkImportStudents') {
    const rows = body.students as Array<{ name?: string; email?: string; phone?: string }>;
    if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) throw new Error('請至少提供一位學員。');
    const existing = data.students.filter(item => item.workshop_id === workshop.id);
    const seenEmails = new Set(existing.map(item => item.email));
    const seenPhones = new Set(existing.map(item => item.phone));
    const next = rows.map(row => ({ name: String(row.name ?? '').trim(), email: String(row.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(), phone: String(row.phone ?? '').trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '') }));
    for (const row of next) {
      if (!row.name || !row.email || !/^09\d{8}$/.test(row.phone) || !/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(row.email)) throw new Error('匯入資料需包含有效的姓名、Email 與手機電話。');
      if (seenEmails.has(row.email) || seenPhones.has(row.phone)) throw new Error(`Email 或手機電話重複：${row.email}`);
      seenEmails.add(row.email); seenPhones.add(row.phone);
    }
    data.students.push(...next.map(row => ({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshop.id, ...row, revision: 0, q1_score: null, q1_rating: null, q2_score: null, q2_rating: null, q3_score: null, q3_rating: null, q4_score: null, q4_rating: null, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email })));
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  if (action === 'saveStudent') {
    const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim(), email = String(body.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(), phone = String(body.phone ?? '').trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '');
    if (!name || !email || !/^09\d{8}$/.test(phone)) throw new Error('請完整填寫有效的姓名、Email 與手機電話。');
    if (data.students.some(item => item !== current && item.workshop_id === workshop.id && (item.email === email || item.phone === phone))) throw new Error('此梯次已有相同的 Email 或手機電話。');
    if (current) { Object.assign(current, { name, email, phone, revision: current.revision + 1, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email }); }
    else data.students.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshop.id, name, email, phone, revision: 0, q1_score: null, q1_rating: null, q2_score: null, q2_rating: null, q3_score: null, q3_rating: null, q4_score: null, q4_rating: null, updated_at: now, updated_by: teacher.email });
    save(data); return { ok: true } as T;
  }
  throw new Error('不支援的操作。');
}
lib/student-return.ts

修改前

（新增檔案，原先不存在）

修改後

import { decodeSurveyCode } from './survey-short-link';
const uuid = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
export function studentReturnUrl(origin: string, base: string, search: string, reset = false) {
  const url = new URL(`${base.replace(/\/$/, '')}/student/`, origin);
  const params = new URLSearchParams(search);
  const survey = params.getAll('survey').length === 1 ? params.get('survey') : null;
  const short = params.getAll('s').length === 1 ? decodeSurveyCode(params.get('s')!) : null;
  const id = survey && uuid.test(survey) ? survey.toLowerCase() : short;
  if (reset) url.searchParams.set('reset', '1');
  if (id) url.searchParams.set('survey', id);
  return url.href;
}
lib/survey-analysis.ts

修改前

import type { SurveyAssignment, SurveyDefinition, SurveyScores, SurveyAnswer } from './surveys';

export type SurveyAnalysisWorkshop = { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null };
export type SurveyAnalysisStudent = { id: string; workshop_id: string; name: string; identity: string };
export type SurveyAnalysisResponse = { assignment_id: string; student_id: string; identity: string; submitted_at: string; answers: SurveyAnswer[]; scores: SurveyScores };
export type SurveyAnalysisData = { includesArchived: boolean; workshops: SurveyAnalysisWorkshop[]; students: SurveyAnalysisStudent[]; assignments: SurveyAssignment[]; responses: SurveyAnalysisResponse[] };
export type SurveyPerson = { identity: string; names: string; studentIds: string[]; workshopIds: string[] };
export type SurveyTrendPoint = { id: string; label: string; date: string; workshop: string; value: number | null; count: number };
export const scaleKey = (a: Pick<SurveyAssignment, 'scale_code' | 'scale_version'>) => JSON.stringify([a.scale_code, a.scale_version]);
export const dimensionLabel = (name: string) => name === 'flexibility' ? '思考彈性' : name === 'structure' ? '記憶中的知識結構' : name;
export function metricValue(scores: SurveyScores, metric: string): number | null {
  const name = metric.startsWith('dimension:') ? metric.slice(10) : metric;
  const value = metric === 'total' ? scores.total : scores.dimensions?.[name] ?? (name === 'flexibility' ? scores.flexibility : name === 'structure' ? scores.structure : undefined);
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}
export function metricRange(definition: SurveyDefinition, metric: string) {
  const name = metric.startsWith('dimension:') ? metric.slice(10) : metric;
  const count = metric === 'total' ? definition.items.length : definition.dimensions?.[name]?.length ?? definition.items.length;
  return { min: count * (definition.minScore ?? 1), max: count * (definition.maxScore ?? 6) };
}
export function orderedAssignments(assignments: SurveyAssignment[]) {
  // Equal timestamps retain the supplied publication order; UUID order is not chronology.
  return [...assignments].sort((a, b) => (Date.parse(a.created_at ?? '') || 0) - (Date.parse(b.created_at ?? '') || 0));
}
export function surveyPeople(data: SurveyAnalysisData, assignments: SurveyAssignment[]): SurveyPerson[] {
  const workshops = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.workshop_id));
  const assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const groups = new Map<string, { names: Set<string>; students: Set<string>; workshops: Set<string> }>();
  const add = (identity: string, s: SurveyAnalysisStudent) => {
    const group = groups.get(identity) ?? { names: new Set<string>(), students: new Set<string>(), workshops: new Set<string>() };
    group.names.add(s.name); group.students.add(s.id); group.workshops.add(s.workshop_id); groups.set(identity, group);
  };
  data.students.filter(s => workshops.has(s.workshop_id)).forEach(s => add(s.identity, s));
  // A historical response keeps its original account identity even if an account is later replaced.
  data.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id)).forEach(r => { const s = data.students.find(s => s.id === r.student_id); if (s) add(r.identity, s); });
  return [...groups].map(([identity, group]) => ({ identity, names: [...group.names].join('／'), studentIds: [...group.students], workshopIds: [...group.workshops] })).sort((a, b) => a.names.localeCompare(b.names, 'zh-Hant-TW-u-co-stroke') || a.identity.localeCompare(b.identity));
}
export function surveyTrend(data: SurveyAnalysisData, assignments: SurveyAssignment[], people: SurveyPerson[], metric: string): SurveyTrendPoint[] {
  const identities = new Set(people.map(p => p.identity));
  return orderedAssignments(assignments).map((a, i) => {
    const values = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && identities.has(r.identity)).map(r => metricValue(r.scores, metric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
    return { id: a.id, label: a.note || `第 ${i + 1} 次發布`, date: a.created_at ?? '', workshop: data.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.name ?? '', value: values.length ? values.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n, 0) / values.length : null, count: values.length };
  });
}

修改後

import type { SurveyAssignment, SurveyDefinition, SurveyScores, SurveyAnswer } from './surveys';

export type SurveyAnalysisWorkshop = { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null };
export type SurveyAnalysisStudent = { id: string; workshop_id: string; name: string; identity: string };
export type SurveyAnalysisResponse = { assignment_id: string; student_id: string; identity: string; submitted_at: string; answers?: SurveyAnswer[]; scores: SurveyScores };
export type SurveyAnalysisData = { includesArchived: boolean; workshops: SurveyAnalysisWorkshop[]; students: SurveyAnalysisStudent[]; assignments: SurveyAssignment[]; responses: SurveyAnalysisResponse[] };
export type SurveyPerson = { identity: string; names: string; studentIds: string[]; workshopIds: string[] };
export type SurveyTrendPoint = { id: string; label: string; date: string; workshop: string; value: number | null; count: number };
export const scaleKey = (a: Pick<SurveyAssignment, 'scale_code' | 'scale_version'>) => JSON.stringify([a.scale_code, a.scale_version]);
export const dimensionLabel = (name: string) => name === 'flexibility' ? '思考彈性' : name === 'structure' ? '記憶中的知識結構' : name;
export function metricValue(scores: SurveyScores, metric: string): number | null {
  const name = metric.startsWith('dimension:') ? metric.slice(10) : metric;
  const value = metric === 'total' ? scores.total : scores.dimensions?.[name] ?? (name === 'flexibility' ? scores.flexibility : name === 'structure' ? scores.structure : undefined);
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}
export function metricRange(definition: SurveyDefinition, metric: string) {
  const name = metric.startsWith('dimension:') ? metric.slice(10) : metric;
  const count = metric === 'total' ? definition.items.length : definition.dimensions?.[name]?.length ?? definition.items.length;
  return { min: count * (definition.minScore ?? 1), max: count * (definition.maxScore ?? 6) };
}
export function orderedAssignments(assignments: SurveyAssignment[]) {
  // Equal timestamps retain the supplied publication order; UUID order is not chronology.
  return [...assignments].sort((a, b) => (Date.parse(a.created_at ?? '') || 0) - (Date.parse(b.created_at ?? '') || 0));
}
export function surveyPeople(data: SurveyAnalysisData, assignments: SurveyAssignment[]): SurveyPerson[] {
  const workshops = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.workshop_id));
  const assignmentIds = new Set(assignments.map(a => a.id));
  const groups = new Map<string, { names: Set<string>; students: Set<string>; workshops: Set<string> }>();
  const add = (identity: string, s: SurveyAnalysisStudent) => {
    const group = groups.get(identity) ?? { names: new Set<string>(), students: new Set<string>(), workshops: new Set<string>() };
    group.names.add(s.name); group.students.add(s.id); group.workshops.add(s.workshop_id); groups.set(identity, group);
  };
  data.students.filter(s => workshops.has(s.workshop_id)).forEach(s => add(s.identity, s));
  // A historical response keeps its original account identity even if an account is later replaced.
  data.responses.filter(r => assignmentIds.has(r.assignment_id)).forEach(r => { const s = data.students.find(s => s.id === r.student_id); if (s) add(r.identity, s); });
  return [...groups].map(([identity, group]) => ({ identity, names: [...group.names].join('／'), studentIds: [...group.students], workshopIds: [...group.workshops] })).sort((a, b) => a.names.localeCompare(b.names, 'zh-Hant-TW-u-co-stroke') || a.identity.localeCompare(b.identity));
}
export function surveyTrend(data: SurveyAnalysisData, assignments: SurveyAssignment[], people: SurveyPerson[], metric: string): SurveyTrendPoint[] {
  const identities = new Set(people.map(p => p.identity));
  return orderedAssignments(assignments).map((a, i) => {
    const values = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && identities.has(r.identity)).map(r => metricValue(r.scores, metric)).filter((n): n is number => n !== null);
    return { id: a.id, label: a.note || `第 ${i + 1} 次發布`, date: a.created_at ?? '', workshop: data.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.name ?? '', value: values.length ? values.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n, 0) / values.length : null, count: values.length };
  });
}
lib/survey-demo.ts

修改前

import definition from './dti-sk.json';
import vpirsDefinition from './vpirs-e.json';
import satisfactionDefinition from './learning-satisfaction.json';
import { scoreSurvey } from './survey-scoring';
import { validateSurveyResponse, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveyDefinition, type SurveyInstrument, type SurveyResponse } from './surveys';

type Context = { user: { role: string; email: string }; workshops: { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null }[]; students: { id: string; workshop_id: string; email: string; name: string }[] };
type DemoResponse = SurveyResponse & { requestId: string; userEmail: string };
type StoredAssignment = SurveyAssignment & { requestId?: string; createdBy?: string };
type State = { library: SurveyInstrument[]; assignments: StoredAssignment[]; responses: DemoResponse[] };
const key = 'nptc-surveys-demo-v1';
const publicDefinition = (d: SurveyDefinition) => { const { reverseItems: _reverse, dimensions: _dimensions, negativeItems: _negative, ...visible } = d; return visible; };

export function demoSurveyRpc(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, ctx: Context) {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem(key);
  const data: State = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : { library: [], assignments: [], responses: [] };
  let migrated = false;
  if (!data.library?.length) { data.library = [{ code: definition.code, version: definition.version, definition }]; migrated = true; }
  if (!data.library.some(i => i.code === vpirsDefinition.code && i.version === vpirsDefinition.version)) {
    data.library.push({ code: vpirsDefinition.code, version: vpirsDefinition.version, definition: vpirsDefinition }); migrated = true;
  }
  if (!data.library.some(i => i.code === satisfactionDefinition.code && i.version === satisfactionDefinition.version)) {
    data.library.push({ code: satisfactionDefinition.code, version: satisfactionDefinition.version, definition: satisfactionDefinition as SurveyDefinition }); migrated = true;
  }
  data.assignments.forEach(a => {
    if (a.note === undefined) { a.note = a.phase === 'pre' ? '前測' : a.phase === 'post' ? '後測' : ''; delete a.phase; migrated = true; }
    if (!a.created_at) { a.created_at = new Date().toISOString(); migrated = true; }
  });
  const save = () => localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
  if (migrated) save();
  const body = (args.body ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const teacher = () => { if (ctx.user.role !== 'teacher') throw new Error('此帳號沒有量表管理權限。'); };
  const workshopId = String(args.requested_workshop ?? body.workshopId ?? '');
  const requireWorkshop = (id: string, write = false) => { const workshop = ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === id); if (!workshop || write && workshop.archived_at) throw new Error('找不到可修改的梯次，或梯次已封存。'); return workshop; };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_library') { teacher(); return { instruments: data.library }; }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_analysis') {
    teacher();
    const requested = args.requested_workshop ? String(args.requested_workshop) : null;
    if (requested) requireWorkshop(requested);
    const workshops = ctx.workshops.filter(w => !w.archived_at);
    const ids = new Set(workshops.filter(w => !requested || w.id === requested).map(w => w.id));
    const assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => ids.has(a.workshop_id));
    const students = ctx.students.filter(s => ids.has(s.workshop_id)).map(s => ({ id: s.id, workshop_id: s.workshop_id, name: s.name, identity: `email:${s.email.trim().toLowerCase()}` }));
    const responses = data.responses.filter(r => assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id)).map(r => ({ assignment_id: r.assignment_id, student_id: r.student_id, identity: `email:${r.userEmail.trim().toLowerCase()}`, submitted_at: r.submitted_at, answers: r.answers, scores: r.scores }));
    return { includesArchived: false, workshops, assignments, students, responses };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_library_save') throw new Error('目前暫不提供新增或修訂量表，請使用量表庫中的既有內容。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_delete_info' || name === 'nptc_survey_delete') {
    teacher();
    const id = String(args.requested_assignment ?? body.id ?? '');
    const assignment = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === id);
    if (!assignment) throw new Error('問卷已刪除或不存在，請更新發布紀錄。');
    requireWorkshop(assignment.workshop_id, name === 'nptc_survey_delete');
    const count = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === id).length;
    if (name === 'nptc_survey_delete_info') return { id, title: assignment.definition.title, revision: assignment.revision, responseCount: count };
    if (body.revision !== assignment.revision) throw new Error('問卷設定已變更，請重新讀取刪除資訊。');
    if (body.expectedResponses !== count) throw new Error('作答筆數已變更，請重新讀取刪除資訊並確認影響範圍。');
    if (count > 0) {
      if (body.acknowledgeDataLoss !== true) throw new Error('請確認永久刪除這些作答與分數。');
      if (String(body.demoEmail ?? '').trim().toLowerCase() !== ctx.user.email.toLowerCase() || body.demoPassword !== 'demo1234') throw new Error('管理員帳號或密碼驗證失敗。');
    }
    data.responses = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id !== id);
    data.assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => a.id !== id);
    save(); return { ok: true, deletedResponses: count };
  }
  if (['nptc_survey_teacher_data', 'nptc_survey_export', 'nptc_survey_assign'].includes(name)) { teacher(); requireWorkshop(workshopId, name === 'nptc_survey_assign'); }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_create_pair') throw new Error('請從量表庫選擇量表並加入梯次。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_assign') {
    const instrument = data.library.find(i => i.code === body.scaleCode && i.version === body.scaleVersion);
    if (!instrument) throw new Error('找不到選擇的量表版本。');
    if (typeof body.requestId !== 'string' || !body.requestId) throw new Error('請重新選擇量表。');
    const note = String(body.note ?? '').trim(), status = body.status === 'draft' ? 'draft' : 'open';
    if (note.length > 2000) throw new Error('備註不可超過 2000 字。');
    const existing = data.assignments.find(a => a.requestId === body.requestId && a.createdBy === ctx.user.email);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.workshop_id !== workshopId || existing.scale_code !== instrument.code || existing.scale_version !== instrument.version) throw new Error('這個加入請求已使用，請重新選擇量表。');
      return { ok: true, id: existing.id };
    }
    const assignment: StoredAssignment = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshopId, note, created_at: now, scale_code: instrument.code, scale_version: instrument.version, definition: structuredClone(instrument.definition), revision: 0, status, opens_at: null, closes_at: null, requestId: body.requestId, createdBy: ctx.user.email };
    data.assignments.push(assignment); save(); return { ok: true, id: assignment.id };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_update') {
    teacher(); const a = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === body.id);
    if (!a) throw new Error('找不到問卷。'); requireWorkshop(a.workshop_id, true);
    if (a.revision !== body.revision) throw new Error('問卷設定已更新，請重新讀取。');
    if (body.status !== 'open' && body.status !== 'closed') throw new Error('請選擇開放或關閉。');
    const opens = body.opensAt ? String(body.opensAt) : null, closes = body.closesAt ? String(body.closesAt) : null, note = String(body.note ?? a.note ?? '').trim();
    if (opens && closes && opens >= closes) throw new Error('截止時間須晚於開放時間。');
    if (note.length > 2000) throw new Error('備註不可超過 2000 字。');
    Object.assign(a, { status: body.status, note, opens_at: opens, closes_at: closes, revision: a.revision + 1 }); save(); return { ok: true };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_teacher_data') {
    const assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => a.workshop_id === workshopId);
    const responses = data.responses.filter(r => assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id));
    return { assignments: assignments.map(a => ({ ...a, submitted_count: responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === a.id).length })), students: ctx.students.filter(s => s.workshop_id === workshopId).map(s => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name })), responses: responses.map(r => ({ assignment_id: r.assignment_id, student_id: r.student_id, submitted_at: r.submitted_at, scores: r.scores })) };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_export') return { workshop: ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === workshopId), participants: ctx.students.filter(s => s.workshop_id === workshopId).map(s => s.id), responses: data.responses.filter(r => data.assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id && a.workshop_id === workshopId)).map(({ requestId: _id, userEmail: _email, phase: _phase, ...r }) => ({ ...r, note: data.assignments.find(a => a.id === r.assignment_id)?.note ?? '' })) };
  if (ctx.user.role !== 'student') throw new Error('請登出後使用本梯次學員的帳號與密碼登入。');
  const target = String(args.requested_assignment ?? body.assignmentId ?? '');
  const student = name === 'nptc_survey_student_data' ? ctx.students.find(s => s.id === args.requested_student && s.email === ctx.user.email) : ctx.students.find(s => s.email === ctx.user.email && data.assignments.some(a => a.id === target && a.workshop_id === s.workshop_id));
  if (!student) throw new Error('找不到本人的梯次名冊，請確認使用的是受邀學員帳號。');
  const availability = (a: SurveyAssignment) => {
    if (data.responses.some(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)) return 'submitted';
    if (a.status !== 'open' || ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.archived_at) return 'closed';
    if (a.opens_at && now < a.opens_at) return 'scheduled';
    if (a.closes_at && now >= a.closes_at) return 'expired';
    return 'open';
  };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_student_data') return { assignments: data.assignments.filter(a => a.workshop_id === student.workshop_id && a.status !== 'draft').map(a => ({ id: a.id, workshop_id: a.workshop_id, title: a.definition.title, created_at: a.created_at, availability: availability(a), submitted_at: data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)?.submitted_at ?? null })) };
  const a = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === target && a.status !== 'draft');
  if (!a) throw new Error('問卷尚未發布或連結無效。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_form') return { workshopName: ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.name ?? '', assignment: { id: a.id, workshop_id: a.workshop_id, scale_code: a.scale_code, scale_version: a.scale_version, status: a.status, revision: a.revision, created_at: a.created_at, opens_at: a.opens_at, closes_at: a.closes_at, definition: publicDefinition(a.definition), availability: availability(a), submitted_at: data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)?.submitted_at ?? null } };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_submit') {
    const existing = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.requestId === body.requestId && existing.userEmail === ctx.user.email && JSON.stringify(existing.answers) === JSON.stringify(body.answers) && a.scale_version === body.version) return { ok: true, submitted_at: existing.submitted_at };
      throw new Error('本次問卷已完成，原始作答不可覆寫。');
    }
    if (availability(a) !== 'open') throw new Error('問卷尚未開放、已關閉或已截止。');
    if (body.version !== a.scale_version) throw new Error('量表版本不符。');
    if (typeof body.requestId !== 'string' || !body.requestId) throw new Error('請重新開啟問卷後提交。');
    validateSurveyResponse(body.answers, a.definition);
    data.responses.push({ assignment_id: a.id, student_id: student.id, scale_code: a.scale_code, scale_version: a.scale_version, submitted_at: now, answers: body.answers, scores: scoreSurvey(a.definition, body.answers), requestId: body.requestId, userEmail: ctx.user.email });
    save(); return { ok: true, submitted_at: now };
  }
  throw new Error('不支援的量表操作。');
}

修改後

import definition from './dti-sk.json';
import vpirsDefinition from './vpirs-e.json';
import satisfactionDefinition from './learning-satisfaction.json';
import { scoreSurvey } from './survey-scoring';
import { validateSurveyResponse, type SurveyAssignment, type SurveyDefinition, type SurveyInstrument, type SurveyResponse } from './surveys';

type Context = { user: { role: string; email: string }; workshops: { id: string; name: string; archived_at?: string | null }[]; students: { id: string; workshop_id: string; email: string; name: string }[] };
type DemoResponse = SurveyResponse & { requestId: string; userEmail: string };
type StoredAssignment = SurveyAssignment & { requestId?: string; createdBy?: string };
type State = { library: SurveyInstrument[]; assignments: StoredAssignment[]; responses: DemoResponse[] };
const key = 'nptc-surveys-demo-v1';
const publicDefinition = (d: SurveyDefinition) => { const { reverseItems: _reverse, dimensions: _dimensions, negativeItems: _negative, ...visible } = d; return visible; };

export function demoSurveyRpc(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, ctx: Context) {
  const stored = localStorage.getItem(key);
  const data: State = stored ? JSON.parse(stored) : { library: [], assignments: [], responses: [] };
  let migrated = false;
  if (!data.library?.length) { data.library = [{ code: definition.code, version: definition.version, definition }]; migrated = true; }
  if (!data.library.some(i => i.code === vpirsDefinition.code && i.version === vpirsDefinition.version)) {
    data.library.push({ code: vpirsDefinition.code, version: vpirsDefinition.version, definition: vpirsDefinition }); migrated = true;
  }
  if (!data.library.some(i => i.code === satisfactionDefinition.code && i.version === satisfactionDefinition.version)) {
    data.library.push({ code: satisfactionDefinition.code, version: satisfactionDefinition.version, definition: satisfactionDefinition as SurveyDefinition }); migrated = true;
  }
  data.assignments.forEach(a => {
    if (a.note === undefined) { a.note = a.phase === 'pre' ? '前測' : a.phase === 'post' ? '後測' : ''; delete a.phase; migrated = true; }
    if (!a.created_at) { a.created_at = new Date().toISOString(); migrated = true; }
  });
  const save = () => localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(data));
  if (migrated) save();
  const body = (args.body ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const teacher = () => { if (ctx.user.role !== 'teacher') throw new Error('此帳號沒有量表管理權限。'); };
  const workshopId = String(args.requested_workshop ?? body.workshopId ?? '');
  const requireWorkshop = (id: string, write = false) => { const workshop = ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === id); if (!workshop || write && workshop.archived_at) throw new Error('找不到可修改的梯次，或梯次已封存。'); return workshop; };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_library') { teacher(); return { instruments: data.library }; }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_analysis_workshops') { teacher(); return { includesArchived: false, workshops: ctx.workshops.filter(w => !w.archived_at) }; }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_response_detail') {
    teacher();
    const a = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === args.requested_assignment); if (!a) throw new Error('找不到問卷。'); requireWorkshop(a.workshop_id);
    const r = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === args.requested_student); if (!r) throw new Error('找不到這筆作答。');
    return { ...r, identity: `email:${r.userEmail.trim().toLowerCase()}` };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_analysis' || name === 'nptc_survey_analysis_summary') {
    teacher();
    const requested = args.requested_workshop ? String(args.requested_workshop) : null;
    if (requested) requireWorkshop(requested);
    const workshops = ctx.workshops.filter(w => !w.archived_at);
    const ids = new Set(workshops.filter(w => !requested || w.id === requested).map(w => w.id));
    const assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => ids.has(a.workshop_id));
    const students = ctx.students.filter(s => ids.has(s.workshop_id)).map(s => ({ id: s.id, workshop_id: s.workshop_id, name: s.name, identity: `email:${s.email.trim().toLowerCase()}` }));
    const responses = data.responses.filter(r => assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id)).map(r => ({ assignment_id: r.assignment_id, student_id: r.student_id, identity: `email:${r.userEmail.trim().toLowerCase()}`, submitted_at: r.submitted_at, answers: r.answers, scores: r.scores }));
    return { includesArchived: false, workshops, assignments, students, responses: name === 'nptc_survey_analysis_summary' ? responses.map(({ answers: _answers, ...r }) => r) : responses };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_library_save') throw new Error('目前暫不提供新增或修訂量表，請使用量表庫中的既有內容。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_delete_info' || name === 'nptc_survey_delete') {
    teacher();
    const id = String(args.requested_assignment ?? body.id ?? '');
    const assignment = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === id);
    if (!assignment) throw new Error('問卷已刪除或不存在，請更新發布紀錄。');
    requireWorkshop(assignment.workshop_id, name === 'nptc_survey_delete');
    const count = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === id).length;
    if (name === 'nptc_survey_delete_info') return { id, title: assignment.definition.title, revision: assignment.revision, responseCount: count };
    if (body.revision !== assignment.revision) throw new Error('問卷設定已變更，請重新讀取刪除資訊。');
    if (body.expectedResponses !== count) throw new Error('作答筆數已變更，請重新讀取刪除資訊並確認影響範圍。');
    if (count > 0) {
      if (body.acknowledgeDataLoss !== true) throw new Error('請確認永久刪除這些作答與分數。');
      if (String(body.demoEmail ?? '').trim().toLowerCase() !== ctx.user.email.toLowerCase() || body.demoPassword !== 'demo1234') throw new Error('管理員帳號或密碼驗證失敗。');
    }
    data.responses = data.responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id !== id);
    data.assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => a.id !== id);
    save(); return { ok: true, deletedResponses: count };
  }
  if (['nptc_survey_teacher_data', 'nptc_survey_export', 'nptc_survey_assign'].includes(name)) { teacher(); requireWorkshop(workshopId, name === 'nptc_survey_assign'); }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_create_pair') throw new Error('請從量表庫選擇量表並加入梯次。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_assign') {
    const instrument = data.library.find(i => i.code === body.scaleCode && i.version === body.scaleVersion);
    if (!instrument) throw new Error('找不到選擇的量表版本。');
    if (typeof body.requestId !== 'string' || !body.requestId) throw new Error('請重新選擇量表。');
    const note = String(body.note ?? '').trim(), status = body.status === 'draft' ? 'draft' : 'open';
    if (note.length > 2000) throw new Error('備註不可超過 2000 字。');
    const existing = data.assignments.find(a => a.requestId === body.requestId && a.createdBy === ctx.user.email);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.workshop_id !== workshopId || existing.scale_code !== instrument.code || existing.scale_version !== instrument.version) throw new Error('這個加入請求已使用，請重新選擇量表。');
      return { ok: true, id: existing.id };
    }
    const assignment: StoredAssignment = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), workshop_id: workshopId, note, created_at: now, scale_code: instrument.code, scale_version: instrument.version, definition: structuredClone(instrument.definition), revision: 0, status, opens_at: null, closes_at: null, requestId: body.requestId, createdBy: ctx.user.email };
    data.assignments.push(assignment); save(); return { ok: true, id: assignment.id };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_update') {
    teacher(); const a = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === body.id);
    if (!a) throw new Error('找不到問卷。'); requireWorkshop(a.workshop_id, true);
    if (a.revision !== body.revision) throw new Error('問卷設定已更新，請重新讀取。');
    if (body.status !== 'open' && body.status !== 'closed') throw new Error('請選擇開放或關閉。');
    const opens = body.opensAt ? String(body.opensAt) : null, closes = body.closesAt ? String(body.closesAt) : null, note = String(body.note ?? a.note ?? '').trim();
    if (opens && closes && opens >= closes) throw new Error('截止時間須晚於開放時間。');
    if (note.length > 2000) throw new Error('備註不可超過 2000 字。');
    Object.assign(a, { status: body.status, note, opens_at: opens, closes_at: closes, revision: a.revision + 1 }); save(); return { ok: true };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_teacher_data') {
    const assignments = data.assignments.filter(a => a.workshop_id === workshopId);
    const responses = data.responses.filter(r => assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id));
    return { assignments: assignments.map(a => ({ ...a, submitted_count: responses.filter(r => r.assignment_id === a.id).length })), students: ctx.students.filter(s => s.workshop_id === workshopId).map(s => ({ id: s.id, name: s.name })), responses: responses.map(r => ({ assignment_id: r.assignment_id, student_id: r.student_id, submitted_at: r.submitted_at, scores: r.scores })) };
  }
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_export') return { workshop: ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === workshopId), participants: ctx.students.filter(s => s.workshop_id === workshopId).map(s => s.id), responses: data.responses.filter(r => data.assignments.some(a => a.id === r.assignment_id && a.workshop_id === workshopId)).map(({ requestId: _id, userEmail: _email, phase: _phase, ...r }) => ({ ...r, note: data.assignments.find(a => a.id === r.assignment_id)?.note ?? '' })) };
  if (ctx.user.role !== 'student') throw new Error('請登出後使用本梯次學員的帳號與密碼登入。');
  const target = String(args.requested_assignment ?? body.assignmentId ?? '');
  const student = name === 'nptc_survey_student_data' ? ctx.students.find(s => s.id === args.requested_student && s.email === ctx.user.email) : ctx.students.find(s => s.email === ctx.user.email && data.assignments.some(a => a.id === target && a.workshop_id === s.workshop_id));
  if (!student) throw new Error('找不到本人的梯次名冊，請確認使用的是受邀學員帳號。');
  const availability = (a: SurveyAssignment) => {
    if (data.responses.some(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)) return 'submitted';
    if (a.status !== 'open' || ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.archived_at) return 'closed';
    if (a.opens_at && now < a.opens_at) return 'scheduled';
    if (a.closes_at && now >= a.closes_at) return 'expired';
    return 'open';
  };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_student_data') return { assignments: data.assignments.filter(a => a.workshop_id === student.workshop_id && a.status !== 'draft').map(a => ({ id: a.id, workshop_id: a.workshop_id, title: a.definition.title, created_at: a.created_at, availability: availability(a), submitted_at: data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)?.submitted_at ?? null })) };
  const a = data.assignments.find(a => a.id === target && a.status !== 'draft');
  if (!a) throw new Error('問卷尚未發布或連結無效。');
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_form') return { workshopName: ctx.workshops.find(w => w.id === a.workshop_id)?.name ?? '', assignment: { id: a.id, workshop_id: a.workshop_id, scale_code: a.scale_code, scale_version: a.scale_version, status: a.status, revision: a.revision, created_at: a.created_at, opens_at: a.opens_at, closes_at: a.closes_at, definition: publicDefinition(a.definition), availability: availability(a), submitted_at: data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id)?.submitted_at ?? null } };
  if (name === 'nptc_survey_submit') {
    const existing = data.responses.find(r => r.assignment_id === a.id && r.student_id === student.id);
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.requestId === body.requestId && existing.userEmail === ctx.user.email && JSON.stringify(existing.answers) === JSON.stringify(body.answers) && a.scale_version === body.version) return { ok: true, submitted_at: existing.submitted_at };
      throw new Error('本次問卷已完成，原始作答不可覆寫。');
    }
    if (availability(a) !== 'open') throw new Error('問卷尚未開放、已關閉或已截止。');
    if (body.version !== a.scale_version) throw new Error('量表版本不符。');
