test('staged secret scan handles large source files and never prints matched credentials',async()=>{
 const script=resolve('scripts/check-secrets.mjs');
 const folder=await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(),'nptc-secret-scan-'));
 try {
  execFileSync('git',['init','-q'],{cwd:folder});
  await writeFile(join(folder,'large.ts'),'// safe source\n'.repeat(100000));execFileSync('git',['add','large.ts'],{cwd:folder});
  let result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[script,'--staged'],{cwd:folder,encoding:'utf8'});assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);
  const token=key('service_role',120);await writeFile(join(folder,'large.ts'),'// safe source\n'.repeat(100000)+token);execFileSync('git',['add','large.ts'],{cwd:folder});
  result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[script,'--staged'],{cwd:folder,encoding:'utf8'});assert.equal(result.status,1);assert.ok(!`${result.stdout}${result.stderr}`.includes(token));assert.ok(result.stderr.includes('large.ts'));
 } finally {
  assert.equal(dirname(resolve(folder)),resolve(tmpdir()));assert.ok(basename(folder).startsWith('nptc-secret-scan-'));await rm(folder,{recursive:true,force:true});
 }
});
tests/student-activate-handler.mjs

修改前

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import { webcrypto } from 'node:crypto';
import ts from 'typescript';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
let handler, consumed=0, created=0, allow=true, createFail=false, resultCode;
const client={rpc:async()=>{consumed++;return {data:{ok:allow,code:resultCode,email:'test@example.invalid'},error:null}},auth:{admin:{createUser:async()=>{created++;return {error:createFail?new Error('fail'):null}}}}};
const source=readFileSync('supabase/functions/student-activate/index.ts','utf8').replace(/^import[\s\S]*?;\r?\n/,'');
vm.runInNewContext(ts.transpileModule(source,{compilerOptions:{target:ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,module:ts.ModuleKind.None}}).outputText,{Response,Request,TextEncoder,btoa,crypto:webcrypto,exports:{},createClient:()=>client,Deno:{env:{get:()=>'test-only-shared-secret-at-least-32-characters'},serve:fn=>handler=fn}});
const body={name:'Test',email:'test@example.invalid',phone:'0912345678',password:'Test-password-123'};
const post=data=>handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)}));
assert.equal((await post({...body,password:'short'})).status,400);assert.equal(consumed,0);
allow=false;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,400);assert.equal(created,0);
resultCode='already_activated';assert.equal((await post(body)).status,422);assert.equal(created,0);
resultCode='account_exists';assert.equal((await post(body)).status,410);assert.equal(created,0);resultCode=undefined;
allow=true;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,200);assert.equal(created,1);
createFail=true;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,409);
assert.equal((await handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'GET'}))).status,405);
console.log('PASS: invalid input rejected before RPC, rejected roster cannot create account, success and creation failure responses');

修改後

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import { webcrypto } from 'node:crypto';
import ts from 'typescript';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
let handler, consumed=0, created=0, allow=true, createFail=false, resultCode;
const client={rpc:async()=>{consumed++;return {data:{ok:allow,code:resultCode,email:'test@example.invalid'},error:null}},auth:{admin:{createUser:async()=>{created++;return {error:createFail?new Error('fail'):null}}}}};
const source=readFileSync('supabase/functions/student-activate/index.ts','utf8').replace(/^import[\s\S]*?;\r?\n/,'');
vm.runInNewContext(ts.transpileModule(source,{compilerOptions:{target:ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,module:ts.ModuleKind.None}}).outputText,{Response,Request,TextEncoder,TextDecoder,btoa,crypto:webcrypto,exports:{},createClient:()=>client,Deno:{env:{get:()=>'test-only-shared-secret-at-least-32-characters'},serve:fn=>handler=fn}});
const body={name:'Test',email:'test@example.invalid',phone:'0912345678',password:'Test-password-123'};
const post=data=>handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(data)}));
assert.equal((await post({...body,password:'short'})).status,400);assert.equal(consumed,0);
allow=false;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,400);assert.equal(created,0);
resultCode='already_activated';assert.equal((await post(body)).status,422);assert.equal(created,0);
resultCode='account_exists';assert.equal((await post(body)).status,410);assert.equal(created,0);resultCode=undefined;
allow=true;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,200);assert.equal(created,1);
createFail=true;assert.equal((await post(body)).status,409);
assert.equal((await handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'GET'}))).status,405);
const previousCalls=consumed;
assert.equal((await handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Length':'99999'},body:'{}'}))).status,413);
assert.equal((await post({...body,name:'漢'.repeat(1500)})).status,413,'limit counts UTF-8 bytes');
let canceled=false;
const stream=new ReadableStream({start(controller){controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(4097));},cancel(){canceled=true;}});
assert.equal((await handler(new Request('https://example.invalid',{method:'POST',body:stream,duplex:'half'}))).status,413);
assert.equal(canceled,true);assert.equal(consumed,previousCalls,'oversized bodies never reach the roster service');
console.log('PASS: invalid input rejected before RPC, rejected roster cannot create account, success and creation failure responses');
tests/survey-short-links.mjs

修改前

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join, basename, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import jsQR from 'jsqr';
import { encodeSurveyId, decodeSurveyCode, shortSurveyPath, surveyIdFromShortQuery } from '../lib/survey-short-link.ts';
import { surveyQrDataUrl } from '../lib/survey-qr.ts';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PNG } = createRequire(require.resolve('qrcode'))('pngjs');
const id = '58a1716d-05b0-46fc-98fe-83cf10d86fe3';
const shortUrl = 'https://nptc-wfh.zeabur.app' + shortSurveyPath(id);

test('all UUID bits round-trip without a database and malformed codes cannot select another destination', () => {
  for (const value of [id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff', ...Array.from({length:100}, () => randomUUID())]) {
    const code = encodeSurveyId(value.toUpperCase());
    assert.equal(code.length, 22);
    assert.equal(code, Buffer.from(value.replaceAll('-', ''), 'hex').toString('base64url'));
    assert.equal(decodeSurveyCode(code), value);
    assert.equal(surveyIdFromShortQuery(`?s=${code}`), value);
  }
  assert.ok(shortUrl.length < (`https://nptc-wfh.zeabur.app/student/?survey=${id}`).length);
  assert.equal(surveyIdFromShortQuery(`?s=${encodeSurveyId(id)}&s=${encodeSurveyId(randomUUID())}`), null);
  for (const bad of ['', 'abc', 'https://example.test', '../teacher', 'A'.repeat(21), 'A'.repeat(23), 'A'.repeat(21)+'B', '%'.repeat(22)]) assert.equal(decodeSurveyCode(bad), null);
  assert.throws(() => encodeSurveyId('not-a-publication'), /問卷編號/);
});

test('downloadable PNG independently decodes to the exact short link', async () => {
  const dataUrl = await surveyQrDataUrl(shortUrl);
  assert.ok(dataUrl.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,'));
  const png = PNG.sync.read(Buffer.from(dataUrl.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
  assert.equal(png.width, 1024); assert.equal(png.height, 1024);
  const decoded = jsQR(new Uint8ClampedArray(png.data), png.width, png.height);
  assert.equal(decoded?.data, shortUrl);
  await assert.rejects(() => surveyQrDataUrl('javascript:alert(1)'), /連結格式/);
  await assert.rejects(() => surveyQrDataUrl('http://example.test/q/'), /連結格式/);
});

test('static short-link landing page exposes questionnaire metadata before JavaScript runs', async () => {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'nptc-short-links-'));
  try {
    await mkdir(join(dir, 'dist'));
    await writeFile(join(dir, 'dist/index.html'), '<html><head><title>課程首頁</title><meta name="description" content="課程介紹"/></head><body><script type="module" src="/nptc/assets/app.js"></script></body></html>');
    const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [resolve('scripts/static-routes.mjs')], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
    const q = await readFile(join(dir, 'dist/q/index.html'), 'utf8');
    assert.ok(q.includes('<title>問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC</title>'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('property="og:title" content="問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC"'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('請使用學員帳號與密碼登入'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('src="/nptc/assets/app.js"'), 'keep subpath build asset URLs');
    assert.ok(!(await readFile(join(dir, 'dist/student/index.html'), 'utf8')).includes('property="og:title"'));
  } finally {
    assert.ok(basename(dir).startsWith('nptc-short-links-'));
    await rm(dir, {recursive:true,force:true});
  }
});

修改後

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join, basename, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import jsQR from 'jsqr';
import { encodeSurveyId, decodeSurveyCode, shortSurveyPath, surveyIdFromShortQuery } from '../lib/survey-short-link.ts';
import { surveyQrDataUrl } from '../lib/survey-qr.ts';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PNG } = createRequire(require.resolve('qrcode'))('pngjs');
const id = '58a1716d-05b0-46fc-98fe-83cf10d86fe3';
const shortUrl = 'https://nptc-wfh.zeabur.app' + shortSurveyPath(id);

test('all UUID bits round-trip without a database and malformed codes cannot select another destination', () => {
  for (const value of [id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', 'ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff', ...Array.from({length:100}, () => randomUUID())]) {
    const code = encodeSurveyId(value.toUpperCase());
    assert.equal(code.length, 22);
    assert.equal(code, Buffer.from(value.replaceAll('-', ''), 'hex').toString('base64url'));
    assert.equal(decodeSurveyCode(code), value);
    assert.equal(surveyIdFromShortQuery(`?s=${code}`), value);
  }
  assert.ok(shortUrl.length < (`https://nptc-wfh.zeabur.app/student/?survey=${id}`).length);
  assert.equal(surveyIdFromShortQuery(`?s=${encodeSurveyId(id)}&s=${encodeSurveyId(randomUUID())}`), null);
  for (const bad of ['', 'abc', 'https://example.test', '../teacher', 'A'.repeat(21), 'A'.repeat(23), 'A'.repeat(21)+'B', '%'.repeat(22)]) assert.equal(decodeSurveyCode(bad), null);
  assert.throws(() => encodeSurveyId('not-a-publication'), /問卷編號/);
});

test('downloadable PNG independently decodes to the exact short link', async () => {
  const dataUrl = await surveyQrDataUrl(shortUrl);
  assert.ok(dataUrl.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,'));
  const png = PNG.sync.read(Buffer.from(dataUrl.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
  assert.equal(png.width, 1024); assert.equal(png.height, 1024);
  const decoded = jsQR(new Uint8ClampedArray(png.data), png.width, png.height);
  assert.equal(decoded?.data, shortUrl);
  await assert.rejects(() => surveyQrDataUrl('javascript:alert(1)'), /連結格式/);
  await assert.rejects(() => surveyQrDataUrl('http://example.test/q/'), /連結格式/);
});

test('static short-link landing page exposes questionnaire metadata before JavaScript runs', async () => {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'nptc-short-links-'));
  try {
    await mkdir(join(dir, 'dist'));
    await writeFile(join(dir, 'dist/index.html'), '<html><head><title>課程首頁</title><meta name="description" content="課程介紹"/></head><body><script type="module" src="/nptc/assets/app.js"></script></body></html>');
    const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [resolve('scripts/static-routes.mjs')], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8' });
    assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stderr);
    const q = await readFile(join(dir, 'dist/q/index.html'), 'utf8');
    assert.ok(q.includes('<title>問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC</title>'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('property="og:title" content="問卷填寫｜為國考而訓 NPTC"'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('請使用學員帳號與密碼登入'));
    assert.ok(q.includes('src="/nptc/assets/app.js"'), 'keep subpath build asset URLs');
    assert.ok(q.includes('http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy"'));
    const metaPolicy=q.match(/http-equiv="Content-Security-Policy" content="([^"]+)"/)[1];
    assert.ok(metaPolicy.includes("script-src 'self'"));assert.ok(!metaPolicy.includes('frame-ancestors'),'frame-ancestors requires an HTTP header');
    const headers=await readFile(join(dir,'dist/_headers'),'utf8');assert.ok(headers.includes('X-Frame-Options: DENY'));assert.ok(headers.includes("frame-ancestors 'none'"));
    assert.ok(!(await readFile(join(dir, 'dist/student/index.html'), 'utf8')).includes('property="og:title"'));
  } finally {
    assert.ok(basename(dir).startsWith('nptc-short-links-'));
    await rm(dir, {recursive:true,force:true});
  }
});
